# Missing receipts as opening stock

**Session ID:** ses_e125607d1ffeePmelwvm7lbuPF
**Created:** 11/18/2026, 12:58:21 PM
**Updated:** 11/19/2026, 12:03:57 PM

---

## User

The "add the missing receipts" fix plan
Problem: raw/packing materials were consumed in production but never recorded as received — no purchase invoice, no opening stock — so their batches sit negative (Aerosil's −3.6 is one instance). At the time it was 54 negative batches, raw batches net ≈ −3.29M.
The decision we landed on:
1. Do NOT add purchase invoices. Party ledgers are already correct, so a purchase invoice would double-hit A/P or cash.
2. Use Items → Opening stock instead — enter the true on-hand quantity + unit cost, leave supplier blank. This creates only a batch + OPENING movement, no AP entry, and no ledger disturbance.
3. GL follow-up: supplier-less opening stock doesn't touch the ledger, so post a Dr Inventory (1200/1210/1220) / Cr Retained Earnings (3100) entry to record the value.
4. Don't double-enter — once added as opening stock, never re-add the same material as a purchase, and don't also run "Fix negative stock" on the same items.
5. After the quantities are entered, re-run the periodic stock correction to clear the remaining negative batches.
Related (also pending from that plan):
- P0 FG relief: Dr COGS / Cr 1220 for the ~7.9M finished-goods overstatement (only 15 of 102 sales posted cost) — waiting on your 30-Nov FG count.
- P2 fill missing item prices via ?unpriced=true.
So the "purchase add" plan, corrected, is: enter the missing receipts as supplier-less opening stock (qty + cost), then one Dr Inventory / Cr Retained Earnings reclass.

479:         if (!item || item.company_id !== this.companyId) {

484:         }
485:         seen.add(item.id);

488:         }

491:         }

493:         const current = await this.batches.getAvailableStock(item.id, this.warehouseId);

501:         if (addedDecimal.isZero()) continue;

532:         );
533: 
534:         const inventoryCode = INVENTORY_ACCOUNT_BY_TYPE[item.item_type] ?? "1200";

600:   /** Saved stock-value corrections, newest first, with their item lines. */
601:   async listStockValues(): Promise<StockValueEntryRow[]> {
602:     const entries = await this.db.all<Omit<StockValueEntryRow, "lines">>(
603:       `SELECT id, company_id, entry_date, journal_entry_id, total_value, created_at, reversed_at
604:          FROM stock_value_entries
605:         WHERE company_id = ?
606:         ORDER BY entry_date DESC, id DESC`,
607:       [this.companyId],
608:     );
609:     if (entries.length === 0) return [];
610:     const lines = await this.db.all<StockValueLineRow>(
611:       `SELECT l.id, l.entry_id, l.item_id, l.batch_id, l.quantity, l.quantity_on_hand, l.unit_cost
612:          FROM stock_value_entry_lines l
613:          JOIN stock_value_entries e ON e.id = l.entry_id
614:         WHERE e.company_id = ?
615:         ORDER BY l.id`,
616:       [this.companyId],
617:     );
618:     const byEntry = new Map<number, StockValueLineRow[]>();
619:     for (const line of lines) {
620:       const list = byEntry.get(line.entry_id);
621:       if (list) list.push(line);
622:       else byEntry.set(line.entry_id, [line]);
623:     }
624:     return entries.map((entry) => ({ ...entry, lines: byEntry.get(entry.id) ?? [] }));
625:   }
626: 
627:   /**
628:    * Undo a saved correction: reverse its journal entry and remove the stock it
629:    * added. Refused once that stock has been consumed/sold — the batch no longer
630:    * holds the full quantity, so a clean undo would corrupt on-hand stock.
631:    */
632:   async reverseStockValues(entryId: number, reversedBy?: number | null): Promise<{ ok: true }> {
633:     const entry = await this.db.get<Omit<StockValueEntryRow, "lines">>(
634:       `SELECT id, company_id, entry_date, journal_entry_id, total_value, created_at, reversed_at
635:          FROM stock_value_entries
636:         WHERE id = ? AND company_id = ?`,
637:       [entryId, this.companyId],
638:     );
639:     if (!entry) throw new NotFoundError(`Stock value correction ${entryId} not found.`);
640:     if (entry.reversed_at) {
641:       throw new ValidationError("This stock value correction was already reversed.");
642:     }
643:     const lines = await this.db.all<StockValueLineRow>(
644:       `SELECT id, entry_id, item_id, batch_id, quantity, quantity_on_hand, unit_cost
645:          FROM stock_value_entry_lines
646:         WHERE entry_id = ?
647:         ORDER BY id`,
648:       [entryId],
649:     );
650: 
651:     return this.db.transaction(async () => {
652:       for (const line of lines) {
653:         const batch = await this.batches.findById(line.batch_id);
654:         if (!batch || dec(batch.quantity_in_stock).plus("0.000001").lessThan(line.quantity)) {
655:           throw new ValidationError(
656:             "Cannot undo: stock from this correction has already been consumed or sold. " +
657:               "Enter a new correction instead.",
658:           );
659:         }
660:       }
661: 
662:       if (entry.journal_entry_id != null) {
663:         const original = await this.journal.findLinesForEntry(entry.journal_entry_id);
664:         if (original.length > 0) {
665:           await this.accounting.postJournalEntry({
666:             voucherType: "OPENING",
667:             entryDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
668:             lines: original.map((line) => ({
669:               accountId: Number(line.account_id),
670:               partyId: line.party_id == null ? null : Number(line.party_id),
671:               description: `Reversal - ${String(line.description ?? "")}`.trim(),
672:               debit: Number(line.credit),
673:               credit: Number(line.debit),
674:             })),
675:             narration: `Reversal of stock value correction #${entryId}`,
676:             sourceTable: "stock_value_entries",
677:             sourceId: entryId,
678:             companyId: this.companyId,
679:           });
680:         }
681:       }
682: 
683:       for (const line of lines) {
684:         await this.batches.adjustQuantity(line.batch_id, -line.quantity);
685:         await insertRow(this.db, "stock_movements", {
686:           item_id: line.item_id,
687:           batch_id: line.batch_id,
688:           warehouse_id: this.warehouseId,
689:           movement_type: "ADJUSTMENT",
690:           quantity: -line.quantity,
691:           unit_cost: line.unit_cost,
692:           movement_date: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
693:           notes: `Stock value correction #${entryId} reversed`,
694:         });
695:       }
696: 
697:       await this.db.run(
698:         "UPDATE stock_value_entries SET reversed_at = datetime('now'), reversed_by = ? WHERE id = ?",
699:         [reversedBy ?? null, entryId],
700:       );
701:       return { ok: true as const };
702:     });
703:   }
704: 
705:   private async validateTaxRate(taxRateId: number | null): Promise<void> {
706:     if (taxRateId == null) return;
707:     const taxRate = await this.taxes.findById(taxRateId);
708:     if (!taxRate) throw new ValidationError("Tax rate does not exist.");
709:     if (taxRate.company_id !== this.companyId) {
710:       throw new ValidationError("Tax rate belongs to different company.");
711:     }
712:     if (taxRate.tax_type !== "SALES_TAX") {
713:       throw new ValidationError("Tax rate must be sales tax type.");
714:     }
715:   }
716: }

Read file /home/oni/Documents/projects/work/BOP-software/web/scripts/post-periodic-stock-correction.ts, lines 1-277
1: // Bring raw + packing inventory on the books down to the physical stock count,
2: // the way the old software does it: opening + purchases − closing = consumed.
3: // The difference becomes COGS. No BOMs involved, so it cannot be fooled by a
4: // wrong component quantity or a missing price.
5: //
6: //   npx tsx scripts/post-periodic-stock-correction.ts                    # dry run
7: //   npx tsx scripts/post-periodic-stock-correction.ts --db fixed         # dry run, other database
8: //   npx tsx scripts/post-periodic-stock-correction.ts --db fixed --apply # post both entries
9: //   ... --date 2026-10-31
10: //   npx tsx scripts/post-periodic-stock-correction.ts --local --apply    # ERP_LOCAL_DB from .env
11: //   ... --local data/erp.sqlite --apply                                  # a specific local file
12: //
13: // In local-primary mode (`ERP_DB_ENGINE=local`) the cloud copy is a replica the
14: // running service overwrites, so the entry has to go into the local file: stop
15: // the service, post with `--local`, start it again.
16: //
17: // Two entries:
18: //   1. Dr 1210 Packing / Cr 3100 Retained Earnings  — opening stock that was
19: //      never valued into the books (OPENING stock movements at current prices).
20: //   2. Dr 5000 COGS / Cr 1200 + 1210 — the rest, so each account lands exactly
21: //      on its counted value.
22: //
23: // Re-running is safe: both entries carry a fixed reference number and are
24: // skipped if already posted. MAX_ADJUSTMENT aborts the run if the gap ever
25: // looks like a data error rather than missing cost.
26: 
27: import { readFileSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
28: import path from "node:path";
29: import type { JournalLineInput } from "../server/src/services/accountingService.js";
30: 
31: const OPENING_REF = "OPENING-STOCK-CORR";
32: const ADJUST_REF = "PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR";
33: const MAX_ADJUSTMENT = 6_000_000;
34: 
35: const args = process.argv.slice(2);
36: const apply = args.includes("--apply");
37: const force = args.includes("--force");
38: const dateArg = args.indexOf("--date");
39: const entryDate = dateArg >= 0 ? args[dateArg + 1] : "2026-10-31";
40: const dbArg = args.indexOf("--db");
41: 
42: /**
43:  * Post into the app's own local SQLite file, where the running service reads
44:  * from. The service must be stopped: it holds the file open and pushes its copy
45:  * over the cloud on start.
46:  */
47: const localArg = args.indexOf("--local");
48: const useLocal = localArg >= 0;
49: if (useLocal) {
50:   process.env.ERP_DB_ENGINE = "local";
51:   const target = args[localArg + 1];
52:   if (target && !target.startsWith("--")) process.env.ERP_LOCAL_DB = target;
53: }

55: /** Point the server's config at another database on the same SQLite Cloud instance. */
56: function retargetDatabase(name: string): void {
57:   const envFile = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..", ".env");
58:   if (!existsSync(envFile)) throw new Error("web/.env not found.");
59:   const line = readFileSync(envFile, "utf8")
60:     .split(/\r?\n/)
61:     .find((raw) => raw.trim().startsWith("SQLITE_CLOUD_URL="));
62:   if (!line) throw new Error("SQLITE_CLOUD_URL is not set in web/.env.");
63:   const url = line.slice(line.indexOf("=") + 1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "");
64:   process.env.ERP_DB_ENGINE = "sqlitecloud";
65:   process.env.SQLITE_CLOUD_URL = url.replace(/(:\d+\/)[^?]*/, `$1${name}`);
66: }
67: 
68: if (dbArg >= 0) retargetDatabase(args[dbArg + 1]);
69: 
70: const TYPES = "'RAW_MATERIAL','PACKING_MATERIAL'";
71: 
72: /** Batch cost when it has one, else the item's purchase price. */
73: const UNIT_COST = `COALESCE(NULLIF(sb.purchase_price,0), i.purchase_price, 0)`;
74: 
75: async function main(): Promise<void> {
76:   // Imported late so a --db override lands before env.ts reads process.env.
77:   const { getDb } = await import("../server/src/db/index.js");
78:   const { env } = await import("../server/src/env.js");
79:   const { AccountingService } = await import("../server/src/services/accountingService.js");
80: 
81:   const db = getDb();
82:   console.log(
83:     `Database: ${
84:       useLocal
85:         ? `${env.localDbPath} (local — the service must be stopped)`
86:         : (process.env.SQLITE_CLOUD_URL ?? "").replace(/apikey=[^&]+/, "apikey=***")
87:     }`,
88:   );
89: 
90:   const balances = new Map<string, number>();
91:   for (const row of await db.all<{ account_code: string; net: number }>(
92:     `SELECT a.account_code, ROUND(SUM(jel.debit - jel.credit), 2) net
93:      FROM journal_entry_lines jel
94:      JOIN accounts a ON a.id = jel.account_id
95:      JOIN journal_entries je ON je.id = jel.journal_entry_id
96:      WHERE je.company_id = 1 AND a.account_code IN ('1200','1210')
97:      GROUP BY a.id`,
98:   )) {
99:     balances.set(row.account_code, Number(row.net));
100:   }
101: 
102:   const opening = new Map<string, number>();
103:   for (const row of await db.all<{ item_type: string; v: number }>(
104:     `SELECT i.item_type,
105:             ROUND(SUM(m.quantity * COALESCE(NULLIF((SELECT MAX(sb.purchase_price) FROM stock_batches sb
106:               WHERE sb.item_id = i.id AND sb.purchase_price > 0), 0), NULLIF(i.purchase_price, 0), 0)), 2) v
107:      FROM stock_movements m
108:      JOIN items i ON i.id = m.item_id
109:      WHERE i.company_id = 1 AND m.movement_type = 'OPENING' AND i.item_type IN (${TYPES})
110:      GROUP BY i.item_type`,
111:   )) {
112:     opening.set(row.item_type, Number(row.v));
113:   }
114: 
115:   const counted = new Map<string, number>();
116:   for (const row of await db.all<{ item_type: string; v: number }>(
117:     `SELECT i.item_type, ROUND(SUM(sb.quantity_in_stock * ${UNIT_COST}), 2) v
118:      FROM stock_batches sb
119:      JOIN items i ON i.id = sb.item_id
120:      WHERE i.company_id = 1 AND sb.quantity_in_stock > 0 AND sb.is_active = 1
121:        AND i.item_type IN (${TYPES})
122:      GROUP BY i.item_type`,
123:   )) {
124:     counted.set(row.item_type, Number(row.v));
125:   }
126: 
127:   // Per-account movement: bring each balance to its counted value, after the
128:   // opening value that was never booked has been added back.
129:   const plan = ["1200", "1210"].map((code) => {
130:     const type = code === "1200" ? "RAW_MATERIAL" : "PACKING_MATERIAL";
131:     const balance = balances.get(code) ?? 0;
132:     const openingValue = opening.get(type) ?? 0;
133:     const target = counted.get(type) ?? 0;
134:     return { code, type, balance, openingValue, target, delta: balance + openingValue - target };
135:   });
136: 
137:   const openingTotal = plan.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.openingValue, 0);
138:   const adjustTotal = plan.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.delta, 0);
139: 
140:   console.log("\n            balance    + opening    - counted   = adjustment");
141:   for (const p of plan) {
142:     console.log(
143:       `  ${p.code} ${p.type.padEnd(16)} ${p.balance.toFixed(2).padStart(12)} ${p.openingValue
144:         .toFixed(2)
145:         .padStart(12)} ${p.target.toFixed(2).padStart(12)} ${p.delta.toFixed(2).padStart(14)}`,
146:     );
147:   }
148:   console.log(`  opening entry total: ${openingTotal.toFixed(2)}`);
149:   console.log(`  COGS adjustment    : ${adjustTotal.toFixed(2)}`);
150: 
151:   if (openingTotal <= 0 && !plan.some((p) => Math.abs(p.delta) >= 0.01)) {
152:     console.log("Nothing to post.");
153:     return;
154:   }
155:   if (Math.abs(adjustTotal) > MAX_ADJUSTMENT && !force) {
156:     throw new Error(
157:       `Adjustment ${adjustTotal.toFixed(2)} exceeds the ${MAX_ADJUSTMENT} cap — check the stock count before posting (use --force to override).`,
158:     );
159:   }
160: 
161:   const accountIds = new Map<string, number>();
162:   for (const row of await db.all<{ id: number; account_code: string }>(
163:     "SELECT id, account_code FROM accounts WHERE company_id = 1 AND account_code IN ('1200','1210','3100','5000')",
164:   )) {
165:     accountIds.set(row.account_code, row.id);
166:   }
167:   const account = (code: string): number => {
168:     const id = accountIds.get(code);
169:     if (!id) throw new Error(`Account ${code} not found.`);
170:     return id;
171:   };
172: 
173:   const posted = new Set(
174:     (
175:       await db.all<{ reference_no: string }>(
176:         "SELECT reference_no FROM journal_entries WHERE company_id = 1 AND reference_no IN (?, ?)",
177:         [OPENING_REF, ADJUST_REF],
178:       )
179:     ).map((r) => r.reference_no),
180:   );
181: 
182:   const jobs: Array<{ ref: string; narration: string; lines: JournalLineInput[] }> = [];
183: 
184:   if (openingTotal > 0 && !posted.has(OPENING_REF)) {
185:     jobs.push({
186:       ref: OPENING_REF,
187:       narration: "Opening stock at 1 October brought into the inventory accounts",
188:       lines: plan
189:         .filter((p) => p.openingValue > 0)
190:         .map((p) => ({
191:           accountId: account(p.code),
192:           debit: p.openingValue,
193:           description: `Opening stock — ${p.type.toLowerCase().replace("_", " ")}`,
194:         }))
195:         .concat([{ accountId: account("3100"), credit: openingTotal, description: "Opening stock" }]),
196:     });
197:   }
198: 
199:   // A negative account delta means the books are *under* the count, so that leg
200:   // is a debit. Building the COGS leg from the signed total keeps the entry
201:   // balanced whichever way each account is off.
202:   if (plan.some((p) => Math.abs(p.delta) >= 0.01) && !posted.has(ADJUST_REF)) {
203:     const lines: JournalLineInput[] = [];
204:     if (Math.abs(adjustTotal) >= 0.01) {
205:       lines.push(
206:         adjustTotal > 0
207:           ? { accountId: account("5000"), debit: adjustTotal, description: "Materials consumed" }
208:           : {
209:               accountId: account("5000"),
210:               credit: -adjustTotal,
211:               description: "Materials consumption reversed — stock over the books",
212:             },
213:       );
214:     }
215:     for (const p of plan) {
216:       if (Math.abs(p.delta) < 0.01) continue;
217:       lines.push({
218:         accountId: account(p.code),
219:         ...(p.delta > 0 ? { credit: p.delta } : { debit: -p.delta }),
220:         description: `Consumed ${p.type.toLowerCase().replace("_", " ")}`,
221:       });
222:     }
223:     jobs.push({
224:       ref: ADJUST_REF,
225:       narration: "Raw and packing materials consumed — opening + purchases less closing stock",
226:       lines,
227:     });
228:   }
229: 
230:   if (jobs.length === 0) {
231:     console.log("Both entries already posted. Nothing to do.");
232:     return;
233:   }
234:   for (const job of jobs) console.log(`  will post ${job.ref} for ${(job.ref === OPENING_REF ? openingTotal : adjustTotal).toFixed(2)}`);
235: 
236:   // Cheap guard before the write: a wrong leg is easier to read here than to
237:   // unwind from the ledger later.
238:   for (const job of jobs) {
239:     const debit = job.lines.reduce((sum, line) => sum + (line.debit ?? 0), 0);
240:     const credit = job.lines.reduce((sum, line) => sum + (line.credit ?? 0), 0);
241:     if (Math.abs(debit - credit) >= 0.01) {
242:       throw new Error(
243:         `${job.ref} is unbalanced: debit ${debit.toFixed(2)} vs credit ${credit.toFixed(2)}.`,
244:       );
245:     }
246:   }
247: 
248:   if (!apply) {
249:     console.log("Dry run — nothing written. Re-run with --apply to post.");
250:     return;
251:   }
252: 
253:   await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
254:     const inside = new AccountingService(tx);
255:     for (const job of jobs) {
256:       const id = await inside.postJournalEntry({
257:         voucherType: "JOURNAL",
258:         entryDate,
259:         narration: job.narration,
260:         referenceNo: job.ref,
261:         lines: job.lines,
262:       });
263:       console.log(`Posted ${job.ref} as entry #${id} on ${entryDate}.`);
264:     }
265:   });
266: }
267: 
268: main()
269:   .then(async () => {
270:     const { closeDb } = await import("../server/src/db/index.js");
271:     await closeDb();
272:     process.exit(0);
273:   })
274:   .catch(async (err) => {
275:     console.error(err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);
276:     process.exit(1);
277:   });

1: // Move the two stock-correction entries to the day the count was actually taken.
2: //
3: // The correction is computed from today's shelf, but it was posted dated
4: // 31 October, so October's closing carries a November fact and November's
5: // opening inherits it. Re-dating it to the count date isolates the two months:
6: // October keeps its own posting, November owns the correction.
7: //
8: //   npx tsx scripts/redate-stock-corrections.ts --local --date 2026-11-15
9: //   npx tsx scripts/redate-stock-corrections.ts --local --date 2026-11-15 --apply
10: //   npx tsx scripts/redate-stock-corrections.ts --db fixed --apply
11: //
12: // Only `entry_date` changes; amounts, lines and totals are untouched.
13: import { readFileSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
14: import path from "node:path";
15: 
16: const REFS = ["OPENING-STOCK-CORR", "PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR"];
17: 
18: const args = process.argv.slice(2);

20: const dateArg = args.indexOf("--date");
21: const newDate = dateArg >= 0 ? args[dateArg + 1] : "";
22: if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(newDate)) {
23:   console.error("Pass --date YYYY-MM-DD (the date the count was taken).");
24:   process.exit(1);
25: }
26: 
27: const dbArg = args.indexOf("--db");
28: const localArg = args.indexOf("--local");
29: const useLocal = localArg >= 0;
30: if (useLocal) {
31:   process.env.ERP_DB_ENGINE = "local";
32:   const target = args[localArg + 1];
33:   if (target && !target.startsWith("--")) process.env.ERP_LOCAL_DB = target;
34: } else if (dbArg >= 0) {
35:   const envFile = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..", ".env");
36:   if (!existsSync(envFile)) throw new Error("web/.env not found.");
37:   const line = readFileSync(envFile, "utf8")
38:     .split(/\r?\n/)
39:     .find((raw) => raw.trim().startsWith("SQLITE_CLOUD_URL="));
40:   if (!line) throw new Error("SQLITE_CLOUD_URL is not set in web/.env.");
41:   const url = line.slice(line.indexOf("=") + 1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "");
42:   process.env.ERP_DB_ENGINE = "sqlitecloud";
43:   process.env.SQLITE_CLOUD_URL = url.replace(/(:\d+\/)[^?]*/, `$1${args[dbArg + 1]}`);
44: }
45: 
46: async function main(): Promise<void> {
47:   const { getDb, closeDb } = await import("../server/src/db/index.js");
48:   const { env } = await import("../server/src/env.js");
49:   const db = getDb();
50:   console.log(
51:     `Database: ${
52:       useLocal
53:         ? `${env.localDbPath} (local — stop the service first)`
54:         : (process.env.SQLITE_CLOUD_URL ?? "").replace(/apikey=[^&]+/, "apikey=***")
55:     }`,
56:   );
57: 
58:   const entries = await db.all<{ id: number; voucher_number: string; entry_date: string; reference_no: string }>(
59:     `SELECT id, voucher_number, entry_date, reference_no FROM journal_entries
60:       WHERE company_id = 1 AND reference_no IN (?, ?) ORDER BY id`,
61:     REFS,
62:   );
63:   if (entries.length === 0) {
64:     console.log("No correction entries found — nothing to re-date.");
65:     await closeDb();
66:     return;
67:   }
68:   for (const entry of entries) {
69:     const verb = entry.entry_date === newDate ? "already" : "move";
70:     console.log(`  ${verb} #${entry.id} ${entry.voucher_number} ${entry.reference_no}: ${entry.entry_date} -> ${newDate}`);
71:   }
72:   if (entries.every((entry) => entry.entry_date === newDate)) {
73:     console.log("Nothing to change.");
74:     await closeDb();
75:     return;
76:   }
77: 
78:   // What the split looks like either side of the move, so the effect is visible
79:   // before it is applied.
80:   const months = await db.all<{ month: string; voucher_type: string; net: number }>(

## Verdict
The corrected plan is right, and — important — **it's already implemented**. There's no new build for the core; this is an execution plan, not a code plan.

## How your plan maps to existing code

**Step 2 — supplier-less opening stock.** `POST /api/items/:id/opening-stock` (`addOpeningStock`, `itemService.ts:373`) creates the batch + `OPENING` movement, and posts a GL entry **only when `partyId` is set** (`itemService.ts:417`). Leave supplier blank → no A/P, no ledger disturbance. Exactly your step 2.

**Step 3 + 5 — the reclass and the consumption.** `scripts/post-periodic-stock-correction.ts` already does *both* in one run:
- `OPENING-STOCK-CORR` → **Dr 1200/1210 / Cr 3100** (value of `OPENING` movements never booked).
- `PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR` → **Dr 5000 / Cr 1200+1210** (bring books to the counted shelf value).

So the "Dr Inventory / Cr Retained Earnings" in step 3 is **not a manual journal** — it's the script's first entry. You enter opening stock (no GL), then the script books the value and the consumption.

## Two things to get right

**1. Don't mix the two reclass mechanisms.** There's also a newer *"Stock value correction"* tool (`setStockValues`, Items page → "Stock value correction" card, `itemService.ts:454`) that posts its own Dr Inv / Cr 3100 per batch. If you use **that** tool *and* then run `post-periodic-stock-correction.ts`, the opening value hits 3100 twice. Pick one path:

- **Recommended (matches your plan verbatim):** supplier-less "Opening stock" form per item → then `post-periodic-stock-correction.ts --apply`. The script is the reclass.
- The "Stock value correction" tool is self-contained, but then you must *not* let the script re-book the opening.

**2. Step 5 wording.** The periodic correction is **GL-only** — it never touches batches. The negative batches are cleared by the *opening-stock entry itself* (step 2), not by the periodic correction. If a batch is still negative after step 2, the entered qty was too low.

## The only possible new code (optional)
54 items one-at-a-time through the single-item form is tedious. A ~15-line script to bulk-insert supplier-less opening stock (batch + `OPENING` movement, no GL) would turn step 2 into one command, leaving the GL entirely to the existing script. That's the only thing that might need writing.

## Sequencing (safety, per your usual)
1. **Read-only pre-flight** — current 1200/1210/3100 net, negative-batch count, and whether `OPENING-STOCK-CORR` / `PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR` / any `stock_value_entries` already exist (decides first-run vs incremental, and which path is safe).
2. Dry-run `post-periodic-stock-correction.ts` (no `--apply`), then backup, then `--apply` on a **test copy** first, then main.

## Unchanged
P0 FG relief (Dr COGS / Cr 1220) and P2 `?unpriced=true` — separate, already tracked, no action here.

53: **Verification (all exit 0, no git writes):** `npm run typecheck` (server +

59: ### 8 of the 12 adversarial-audit defects are FIXED (with regression tests)
60: All are covered by the **new** `web/server/tests/fixes.test.ts` (10 tests) plus
61: updated `tests/banking.test.ts`:
62: 
63: | # | Defect | Fix |
64: |---|---|---|
65: | F1 | `nextVoucherNumber` off-by-one → item creation returned 500 | `journalRepository.ts`: a new sequence starts at **2** and returns **`next_number - 1`** |
66: | F2 | document number ≠ its ledger voucher number, and gaps | the allocated document number is now passed into `postJournalEntry` (sales, purchases, both returns) |
67: | F3 | silent ≥2^63 corruption at the DB driver boundary | `domain/money.ts`: `assertStorageRange()` + `MAX_STORAGE_VALUE = 1e15` in `toStorage`/`toQuantity` |
68: | F4 | `stock_movements` not maintained | movements now inserted on purchase, sale, both returns, production consume/output and opening stock |
69: | F5 | sub-cent quantities rounded to 0 | `toQuantity()` (6 dp) used for every quantity column |
70: | F6 | cash/bank could go arbitrarily negative | funds guard in `bankingService` (withdraw, clear cheque) and `paymentService` (BANK/CHEQUE) |
71: | F9 | returns reversed the wrong COGS account | `returnsService` credits the account the sale debited (5000/5001/5002 by item type) |
72: | F10 | `referenceNo` accepted but never stored | persisted on `journal_entries.reference_no` |
73: | F11 | no payment/receipt idempotency (same payment posted twice) | `paymentRepository.findDuplicate()` + a `ConflictError` (409) guard in `paySupplier`/`receivePayment`, keyed on company/party/date/method/amount/reference |
74: 
75: **F2 also needed a data repair.** `db/bootstrap.ts` now exports
76: `reconcileNumberingSequences()` (advances each sequence to
77: `max(existing document code, journal voucher) + 1`, forward-only, idempotent);
78: it runs at bootstrap, and there is a runnable **`npm -w @bop-erp/server run
79: reconcile`** (`server/src/reconcile.ts`, new file). It was applied once to the
80: hosted DB during the fix session.

## Assistant

Now I can answer with the full accounting chain, not just the mechanism.

## The one-line verdict
Yes — this plan fixes the **raw/packing input leg** of manufacturing cost and clears the negative batches. It does **not** fix the finished-goods leg; that's your P0, and it's the *other half* of "manufacturing cost." Nothing in this plan touches production itself, and it shouldn't — production is already posting correctly.

## The accounting chain (why this is two separate fixes)

A raw material's full lifecycle is three journal legs:

1. **Receive** raw → `Dr 1200 / Cr 2000` (A/P) — **MISSING** (this is the negative batch)
2. **Consume** in production → `Dr 1220 / Cr 1200` (manufacturing, `MANUFACTURING` voucher) — **already posted**
3. **Sell** FG → `Dr 5000 / Cr 1220` (COGS) — **only 15/102 posted** (your P0)

Because leg 1 is missing but leg 2 posted, `1200` goes negative by the consumed value, and that value sits inflated in `1220` (never relieved by leg 3).

## What this plan actually fixes

| Concern | Fixed by this plan? | Entry |
|---|---|---|
| Negative raw/packing batches | ✅ | opening stock (batch + `OPENING` movement) |
| Raw/packing GL value (1200/1210) | ✅ | `OPENING-STOCK-CORR`: Dr 1200/1210 / Cr 3100 |
| Raw/packing consumption true-up | ✅ | `PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR`: Dr 5000 / Cr 1200+1210 |
| **Production** | ✅ (no change) | already `Dr 1220 / Cr 1200/1210` |
| **Finished goods overstatement (~7.9M)** | ❌ **not here** | P0: Dr 5000 / Cr 1220 |

So the plan + your P0 together fully reconcile the TB. The plan alone leaves `1220` overstated and `5000` understated by the FG amount — which is exactly why P0 is still pending on the 30-Nov count.

## Two things that would break it if missed

1. **Don't mix the reclass paths.** The "Stock value correction" tool posts its own `Dr Inv / Cr 3100` *and* creates `OPENING` movements. If you use it *and* run `post-periodic-stock-correction.ts`, the script's `OPENING-STOCK-CORR` re-books the same opening value → `3100` double-counts. Use the **supplier-less "Opening stock" form (no GL) + the script**, exactly as your plan states.

2. **A fully-consumed, never-received material nets to zero on the count**, so the periodic report's `opening + purchases − closing` can read `consumed = 0` for it — the consumption is already sitting in `1220`, not in `5000`. That's not a bug in this plan; it's the reason the FG relief (P0) is what finally pushes that cost into COGS.

---

*Skipped: live figures — I traced the code paths, not the current 1200/1210/1220/5000 balances, so I can't confirm the exact −3.29M / 7.9M reconciliation without reading the DB. Risk: if any of those 54 items is actually a **purchase-voucher** case rather than a missing receipt, opening stock would still double-hit A/P — the plan's correctness hinges on every one of them truly having no supplier invoice.*

=== .env (redacted) ===
# Copy to web/.env and adjust. Never commit real credentials.
#
# web/.env is the complete configuration for the web app. It is independent of
# the Python app's python/.env — there is no cross-app fallback.

# Which database backend the server uses:
#   local       -> a local SQLite file (development, tests) at ERP_LOCAL_DB
#   sqlitecloud -> the hosted SQLite Cloud database (the `main` database)
# This project runs against `main`: no schema changes and no replica push.
ERP_DB_ENGINE=local

# Path to the local SQLite file used when ERP_DB_ENGINE=local
ERP_LOCAL_DB=./data/erp.sqlite

# Connection string for the hosted SQLite Cloud database. In local-primary
# mode this is the REPLICA the server pushes to — nothing is ever read from
# it, so never edit it in the SQLite Cloud dashboard (the next push
# overwrites it). Leave empty to run with no cloud copy.
# Format: sqlitecloud://user:password@host:port/database
SQLITE_CLOUD_URL=sqlitecloud://cjja8z6pvz.g4.sqlite.cloud:8860/fixed?apikey=***

# Scratch database used by `npm run dev:testdb` (safe to write to).
SQLITE_CLOUD_TESTDB_URL=

# --- Cloud replica (local-primary mode) -------------------------------------
# The local database is pushed to SQLITE_CLOUD_URL at startup, at shutdown,
# on demand (Settings → Cloud copy) and every N minutes (default 15). Each
# push replaces the whole cloud database atomically. Set ERP_REPLICA_DRY_RUN=1
# to build the push script and log its size without sending anything.
ERP_REPLICA_MINUTES=5
# ERP_REPLICA_DRY_RUN=1
# Where pushes stage their temporary snapshot.
# ERP_REPLICA_DIR=./backups/replica

# --- HTTP server ------------------------------------------------------------
# Bind address. 0.0.0.0 exposes the API on the network; 127.0.0.1 is for a
# reverse proxy in front of it.
ERP_HOST=127.0.0.1
PORT=4000

# --- Production single-process mode (Windows service) ------------------------
# Serve the built React client from this same process (one port, one service).
# Requires `npm run build` first. Leave 0 in development: Vite serves the client.
ERP_SERVE_CLIENT=1

# Where the built client lives (relative to web/).
# ERP_CLIENT_DIST=./client/dist

# Optional HTTPS: set BOTH to have this process terminate TLS itself.
# ERP_TLS_CERT=./certs/erp.crt
# ERP_TLS_KEY=***

# --- Backups and the live local copy ---------------------------------------
# Folder for snapshots, named by bare UTC timestamp (<YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS>.db).
# ERP_BACKUP_DIR=./backups
# Named snapshots to keep (newest first). 0 keeps everything.
# ERP_BACKUP_KEEP=7
# Every snapshot is also copied to this folder (default: the user's Desktop);
# for an off-machine copy point it at a mapped/shared folder, e.g.
# ERP_DESKTOP_DIR=\\your-pc\bop-backup
# ERP_DESKTOP_DIR=./backups/app-copy
# Optional extra folder for named snapshots, e.g. a NAS or your PC's shared
# folder: ERP_PC_MIRROR_DIR=\\your-pc\bop-backup (or /mnt/nas/bop).
# Auto-backup interval in hours. 0 disables the scheduler; 1 with
# ERP_BACKUP_KEEP=168 gives one snapshot an hour for a week.
ERP_AUTO_BACKUP_HOURS=1
ERP_BACKUP_KEEP=168
# Live local copy: continuously rewritten after writes. Redundant in
# local-primary mode (the database itself is already local) — leave off.
ERP_LIVE_MIRROR=0
ERP_MIRROR_DEBOUNCE_SECONDS=15
# Push the live copy to your computer (mapped drive or UNC share).
# ERP_PC_MIRROR_DIR=\\your-pc\bop-backup
ERP_DESKTOP_DIR=./backups/app-copy

## My second question, in plain terms

The Nov-15 fix (entries 859 + 860) is **already in the books**. When I add the new correction for the current state, there are two ways to handle those old entries:

- **(A) Leave them, post one new adjustment** for just the remaining gap → the Nov-15 fix and today's fix both stay visible in the ledger. Reversible. **← recommended**
- **(B) Reverse 859/860, then post one clean correction** → tidier final ledger, but adds reversal entries and a bigger change.

I recommend **A** — it's safer and reversible, and posting the delta to reach the target is correct either way.

## One thing you should know before we run

The UI "Opening stock" tool creates a **new** batch; it does **not** zero the existing negative batch row. So "bring negatives to zero" needs a **small script** that sets each negative batch's quantity to 0 and records the missing receipt as an `OPENING` movement. The UI alone won't clear the 109 negative rows.

And "for now" = skip pricing, so the 19 unpriced batches get **0 value** — quantity fixed, but their cost stays understated until you do P2 later.

## So the short plan

1. Backup + stop service.
2. Script: every negative batch → 0 (OPENING movement).
3. Post one new correction (Option A) for the priced value + true-up.
4. 10 FG negatives → 0 the same way.
5. FG relief after your 30-Nov count.

=== reverseManualJournal ===
  async listManualJournals(options: DateRangeOptions = {}): Promise<Record<string, unknown>[]> {
    return this.journal.listManual(this.companyId, options.from, options.to);
  }

  /**
   * Post the opposite of a manual voucher on a chosen date. Posted entries are
   * immutable, so reversal is the correction path (Zoho/ERPNext behaviour).
   * Entries owned by another document (expenses, banking, assets) are refused —
   * those are corrected through their own module.
   */
  async reverseManualJournal(
    id: number,
    input: { reversalDate?: string; narration?: string | null; createdBy?: number | null } = {},
  ): Promise<ManualJournalResult> {
    const companyId = this.companyId;
    const entry = await this.journal.findById(id);
    if (!entry) throw new NotFoundError(`Journal entry ${id} not found.`);
    if (entry.voucher_type !== "JOURNAL" || entry.source_table !== "journal_entries") {
      throw new ValidationError(
        "Only manual journal vouchers can be reversed here — correct the source document instead.",
      );
    }

    const original = await this.journal.findLinesForEntry(id);
    const reversalDate = input.reversalDate || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

    return this.db.transaction(async () => {
      const reversalId = await this.postJournalEntry({
        voucherType: "JOURNAL",
        entryDate: reversalDate,
        referenceNo: String(entry.voucher_number),
        narration: input.narration ?? `Reversal of ${entry.voucher_number}`,
        lines: original.map((line) => ({
          accountId: Number(line.account_id),
          partyId: line.party_id == null ? null : Number(line.party_id),
          description: (line.description as string | null) ?? null,
          debit: Number(line.credit),
          credit: Number(line.debit),
        })),
        sourceTable: "journal_entries",
        companyId,
        createdBy: input.createdBy ?? null,
      });
      await this.db.run("UPDATE journal_entries SET source_id = ? WHERE id = ?", [
        reversalId,
        reversalId,
      ]);
      const row = await this.journal.findById(reversalId);
      return { id: reversalId, voucherNumber: String(row?.voucher_number ?? "") };
    });
  }

  /**
   * Any account may receive journal lines, headers with sub-accounts included:
   * a header posting and its children's postings are separate lines, so a
   * subtree total is still the plain sum of the lines underneath it.

# /etc/systemd/system/bop-erp.service
# BOP ERP — one Node process serving the API and the built React client.
#
# Install:
#   sudo cp deploy/linux/bop-erp.service /etc/systemd/system/
#   sudo systemctl daemon-reload
#   sudo systemctl enable --now bop-erp
#
# Adjust User/WorkingDirectory if the checkout lives elsewhere.
[Unit]
Description=BOP ERP (API + built client on one port)
Wants=network-online.target tailscaled.service
After=network-online.target tailscaled.service

[Service]
Type=simple
User=oni
WorkingDirectory=/home/oni/accounting-app/BOP-software/web

# Pull, rebuild and refresh the local database before serving. The leading '-'
# matters: if the network is down or the build fails, systemd carries on and
# serves the last good build instead of leaving the site down.
ExecStartPre=-/home/oni/accounting-app/BOP-software/web/deploy/linux/update.sh --boot
ExecStart=/usr/bin/node server/dist/index.js

Restart=always
RestartSec=10
Environment=NODE_ENV=production
# The boot update may build and pull the database; x86 laptop, cold cache.
TimeoutStartSec=900

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target

=== start.sh head ===
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# ===========================================================================
#  Daily start file for Linux - the twin of start.bat.
#
#  - pulls the latest code from git (skipped when offline or not a repo;
#    never stashes or discards local changes - it asks instead)
#  - checks that the npm dependencies are installed (installs them if not,
#    or if the update changed package-lock.json)
#  - creates web/.env from .env.example when it is missing
#  - pulls the latest data from the cloud maindb.db into data/erp.sqlite
#  - starts the API + client (npm run dev)
#  - opens the app in the default browser once the client answers
#
#  Run with:  ./start.sh        Ctrl+C stops the app.
#  For a fresh machine install Node.js 22 LTS or newer first.
# ===========================================================================
set -u
cd "$(dirname "$0")" || exit 1

fail() { echo "$@"; read -r -p "Press Enter to close... " _; exit 1; }

if ! command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1 || ! command -v npm >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo
    echo "[X] Node.js / npm was not found on this machine."
    echo "    Install Node.js 22 LTS or newer from https://nodejs.org/en/download"
    echo
    command -v xdg-open >/dev/null 2>&1 && xdg-open "https://nodejs.org/en/download"
    exit 1
fi

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# ===========================================================================
#  Pull code, rebuild, refresh the local database from SQLite Cloud, restart.
#
#    ./deploy/linux/update.sh               # normal deploy (cloud -> local)
#    ./deploy/linux/update.sh --push        # local -> cloud: keep the local
#                                           # database and push it to SQLite Cloud
#    ./deploy/linux/update.sh --skip-tests  # hotfix
#    ./deploy/linux/update.sh --boot        # systemd ExecStartPre: no tests, no restart
#
#  Deliberately NOT `set -e`: a failed pull, install or build must leave the
#  previous build serving rather than take the site down.
#
#  Never touches web/.env, web/data/ or web/backups/ — git ignores all three.
# ===========================================================================
set -uo pipefail

SERVICE="bop-erp"
WEB_DIR=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/../.." && pwd)
REPO_DIR=$(cd "$WEB_DIR/.." && pwd)

SKIP_TESTS=0
BOOT=0
PUSH_DB=0
for arg in "$@"; do
    case "$arg" in
        --skip-tests|-t) SKIP_TESTS=1 ;;
        --boot)          BOOT=1; SKIP_TESTS=1 ;;
        --push|-p)       PUSH_DB=1 ;;
        *) echo "[update] unknown option: $arg"; exit 2 ;;
    esac
done

log() { echo "[update] $*"; }

# Read a key from web/.env without sourcing it (values may contain spaces).
envval() {
    [ -f "$WEB_DIR/.env" ] || return 0
    grep -E "^$1=" "$WEB_DIR/.env" | tail -1 | cut -d= -f2- | sed -E 's/^["'\'']//; s/["'\'']$//'
}

PORT=$(envval PORT);                 PORT=${PORT:-4000}
DB_FILE=$(envval ERP_LOCAL_DB);      DB_FILE=${DB_FILE:-./data/erp.sqlite}
case "$DB_FILE" in /*) ;; *) DB_FILE="$WEB_DIR/$DB_FILE" ;; esac
DATA_DIR=$(dirname "$DB_FILE")
mkdir -p "$DATA_DIR"

# --- 1. code ---------------------------------------------------------------
BEFORE=""
if [ -d "$REPO_DIR/.git" ]; then
    BEFORE=$(git -C "$REPO_DIR" rev-parse HEAD 2>/dev/null || echo "")
    git -C "$REPO_DIR" fetch --quiet || log "fetch failed (offline?) - using the current code"
    git -C "$REPO_DIR" pull --ff-only --quiet || \
        log "pull failed (local changes or divergence) - using the current code"
fi
AFTER=$(git -C "$REPO_DIR" rev-parse HEAD 2>/dev/null || echo "")
CHANGED_FILES=""
if [ -n "$BEFORE" ] && [ -n "$AFTER" ] && [ "$BEFORE" != "$AFTER" ]; then
    CHANGED_FILES=$(git -C "$REPO_DIR" diff --name-only "$BEFORE" "$AFTER")
    log "updated $BEFORE -> $AFTER"
fi

# --- 2. dependencies -------------------------------------------------------
NEED_INSTALL=0
[ -d "$WEB_DIR/node_modules" ] || NEED_INSTALL=1
if printf '%s' "$CHANGED_FILES" | grep -q "package-lock.json"; then NEED_INSTALL=1; fi
if [ "$NEED_INSTALL" = 1 ]; then
    log "installing dependencies"
    ( cd "$WEB_DIR" && (npm ci --no-audit --no-fund || npm install --no-audit --no-fund) ) || \
        log "package install FAILED - continuing with what is installed"
fi

# --- 3. build --------------------------------------------------------------
NEED_BUILD=0
[ -n "$CHANGED_FILES" ] && NEED_BUILD=1
[ -f "$WEB_DIR/server/dist/index.js" ]    || NEED_BUILD=1
[ -f "$WEB_DIR/client/dist/index.html" ]  || NEED_BUILD=1
BUILD_OK=1
if [ "$NEED_BUILD" = 1 ]; then
    log "building"
    ( cd "$WEB_DIR" && npm run build ) || { BUILD_OK=0; log "BUILD FAILED"; }
else
    log "code unchanged - skipping build"
fi
if [ "$BUILD_OK" = 0 ] && [ "$BOOT" = 0 ]; then
    log "not restarting: a failed build must not replace a working one"
    exit 1
fi

# --- 4. tests --------------------------------------------------------------

# --- 4. tests --------------------------------------------------------------
if [ "$SKIP_TESTS" = 0 ] && [ "$BUILD_OK" = 1 ]; then
    log "running tests"
    ( cd "$WEB_DIR" && npm test ) || { log "TESTS FAILED - not deploying"; exit 1; }
fi

# --- 5. stop (so the shutdown push to SQLite Cloud lands first) ------------
if [ "$BOOT" = 0 ]; then
    log "stopping $SERVICE"
    sudo -n systemctl stop "$SERVICE" 2>/dev/null || \
        sudo systemctl stop "$SERVICE" || log "could not stop $SERVICE - continuing"
fi

# --- 6. database -----------------------------------------------------------
# Default: the cloud copy is master, so a deploy replaces the local file with
# it. `--push` flips that for this deploy: the local file is kept and pushed to
# SQLite Cloud instead (whole database, one transaction).
#
# In local-primary mode (ERP_DB_ENGINE=local) the boot update never pulls: the
# `systemctl start` below runs this script with --boot, and pulling there would
# overwrite the local file (and undo a --push) immediately after the deploy.
DB_ENGINE=$(envval ERP_DB_ENGINE); DB_ENGINE=${DB_ENGINE:-sqlitecloud}
DO_PULL=1
[ "$PUSH_DB" = 1 ] && DO_PULL=0
[ "$BOOT" = 1 ] && [ "$DB_ENGINE" = "local" ] && DO_PULL=0

if [ "$PUSH_DB" = 1 ]; then
    log "pushing the local database to SQLite Cloud (no pull)"
    if ( cd "$WEB_DIR" && node scripts/push-cloud.mjs --db "$DB_FILE" ); then
        log "local database pushed to SQLite Cloud"
    else
        log "cloud push FAILED - starting on the local database; the service retries at startup"
    fi
elif [ "$DO_PULL" = 0 ]; then
    log "local-primary mode - keeping the local database (no cloud pull)"
else
    # A previous local file is kept as erp.pre-sync-*.sqlite (3 newest) so a pull
    # against the wrong cloud state is recoverable.
    if [ -f "$DB_FILE" ]; then
        PRE="$DATA_DIR/erp.pre-sync-$(date -u +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S).sqlite"
        cp -f "$DB_FILE" "$PRE" && log "kept previous database as $(basename "$PRE")"
        ls -1t "$DATA_DIR"/erp.pre-sync-*.sqlite 2>/dev/null | tail -n +4 | xargs -r rm -f
    fi
    PULL="$DATA_DIR/erp.pull.sqlite"
    rm -f "$PULL" "$PULL-wal" "$PULL-shm"
    if ( cd "$WEB_DIR" && node scripts/pull-cloud.mjs --out "$PULL" ); then
        mv -f "$PULL" "$DB_FILE"
        # A leftover -wal/-shm from the replaced file corrupts the new one.
        rm -f "$DB_FILE-wal" "$DB_FILE-shm"
        log "local database replaced with the SQLite Cloud copy"
    else
        rm -f "$PULL" "$PULL-wal" "$PULL-shm"
        log "cloud pull FAILED - starting on the existing local database"
    fi
fi

# --- 7. start --------------------------------------------------------------
if [ "$BOOT" = 1 ]; then
    exit 0
fi

log "starting $SERVICE"
sudo -n systemctl start "$SERVICE" 2>/dev/null || sudo systemctl start "$SERVICE" || {
    log "start FAILED - check: journalctl -u $SERVICE -n 50"
    exit 1
}

for _ in $(seq 1 30); do
    if curl -fsS "http://127.0.0.1:$PORT/api/health" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        log "deploy OK - http://127.0.0.1:$PORT/api/health"
        exit 0
    fi
    sleep 1
done

log "health check FAILED after 30s - check: journalctl -u $SERVICE -n 50"
exit 1

/**
 * Local SQLite adapter built on Node's built-in `node:sqlite` module.
 *
 * Used for development and for the fast, network-free test suite. It exposes
 * the exact same `SqlDatabase` contract as the SQLite Cloud adapter so the
 * services and repositories are completely unaware of which one is active.
 *
 * Local SQLite cannot idle-drop the way a remote socket can, but we still
 * provide the same health / interrupt surface so the rest of the app is
 * engine-agnostic.
 */
import { AsyncLocalStorage } from "node:async_hooks";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
import { DatabaseError } from "../domain/errors.js";
import type { Row, RunResult, SqlDatabase, SqlValue } from "./types.js";

type BindValue = string | number | bigint | Uint8Array | null;

/**
 * Re-entrancy marker for `transaction()`: statements issued *inside* a
 * transaction body bypass the serialization queue (they already hold it), and
 * a nested `transaction()` joins the outer one.
 */
const txContext = new AsyncLocalStorage<boolean>();

function bindAll(params?: readonly SqlValue[]): BindValue[] {
  if (!params) return [];
  return params.map((p) => (p === undefined ? null : p));
}

export class LocalSqliteDatabase implements SqlDatabase {
  private readonly db: DatabaseSync;
  /** Serializes every operation on this connection; a transaction body holds it. */
  private chain: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve();

  constructor(file: string) {
    if (file !== ":memory:") {
      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });
    }
    this.db = new DatabaseSync(file);
    this.db.exec("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON");
    this.db.exec("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000");
    // WAL: external readers (DB Browser, scripts) see a consistent snapshot
    // while the app writes, and never block it. File copies still need the
    // -wal sidecar, so backups go through VACUUM INTO instead of `cp`.
    this.db.exec("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL");
    this.db.exec("PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL");
  }

  /** Serialize operations unless we are already inside the held transaction. */
  private schedule<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    if (txContext.getStore()) return fn();
    const run = this.chain.then(fn, fn);
    this.chain = run.then(
      () => undefined,
      () => undefined,
    );
    return run;
  }

  async all<T = Row>(sql: string, params?: readonly SqlValue[]): Promise<T[]> {
    return this.schedule(async () => {
      try {
        return this.db.prepare(sql).all(...bindAll(params)) as T[];
      } catch (err) {
        throw new DatabaseError(messageOf(err), { sql });
      }
    });

277 scripts/post-periodic-stock-correction.ts
// Bring raw + packing inventory on the books down to the physical stock count,
// the way the old software does it: opening + purchases − closing = consumed.
// The difference becomes COGS. No BOMs involved, so it cannot be fooled by a
// wrong component quantity or a missing price.
//
//   npx tsx scripts/post-periodic-stock-correction.ts                    # dry run
//   npx tsx scripts/post-periodic-stock-correction.ts --db fixed         # dry run, other database
//   npx tsx scripts/post-periodic-stock-correction.ts --db fixed --apply # post both entries
//   ... --date 2026-10-31
//   npx tsx scripts/post-periodic-stock-correction.ts --local --apply    # ERP_LOCAL_DB from .env
//   ... --local data/erp.sqlite --apply                                  # a specific local file
//
// In local-primary mode (`ERP_DB_ENGINE=local`) the cloud copy is a replica the
// running service overwrites, so the entry has to go into the local file: stop
// the service, post with `--local`, start it again.
//
// Two entries:
//   1. Dr 1210 Packing / Cr 3100 Retained Earnings  — opening stock that was
//      never valued into the books (OPENING stock movements at current prices).
//   2. Dr 5000 COGS / Cr 1200 + 1210 — the rest, so each account lands exactly
//      on its counted value.
//
// Re-running is safe: both entries carry a fixed reference number and are
// skipped if already posted. MAX_ADJUSTMENT aborts the run if the gap ever
// looks like a data error rather than missing cost.

import { readFileSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import type { JournalLineInput } from "../server/src/services/accountingService.js";

const OPENING_REF = "OPENING-STOCK-CORR";
const ADJUST_REF = "PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR";
const MAX_ADJUSTMENT = 6_000_000;

const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const apply = args.includes("--apply");
const force = args.includes("--force");
const dateArg = args.indexOf("--date");
const entryDate = dateArg >= 0 ? args[dateArg + 1] : "2026-10-31";
const dbArg = args.indexOf("--db");

/**
 * Post into the app's own local SQLite file, where the running service reads
 * from. The service must be stopped: it holds the file open and pushes its copy
 * over the cloud on start.
 */
const localArg = args.indexOf("--local");
const useLocal = localArg >= 0;
if (useLocal) {
  process.env.ERP_DB_ENGINE = "local";
  const target = args[localArg + 1];
  if (target && !target.startsWith("--")) process.env.ERP_LOCAL_DB = target;
}

/** Point the server's config at another database on the same SQLite Cloud instance. */
function retargetDatabase(name: string): void {
  const envFile = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, "..", ".env");
  if (!existsSync(envFile)) throw new Error("web/.env not found.");
  const line = readFileSync(envFile, "utf8")
    .split(/\r?\n/)
    .find((raw) => raw.trim().startsWith("SQLITE_CLOUD_URL="));
  if (!line) throw new Error("SQLITE_CLOUD_URL is not set in web/.env.");
  const url = line.slice(line.indexOf("=") + 1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "");
  process.env.ERP_DB_ENGINE = "sqlitecloud";
  process.env.SQLITE_CLOUD_URL = url.replace(/(:\d+\/)[^?]*/, `$1${name}`);
}

if (dbArg >= 0) retargetDatabase(args[dbArg + 1]);

const TYPES = "'RAW_MATERIAL','PACKING_MATERIAL'";

/** Batch cost when it has one, else the item's purchase price. */
const UNIT_COST = `COALESCE(NULLIF(sb.purchase_price,0), i.purchase_price, 0)`;

async function main(): Promise<void> {
  // Imported late so a --db override lands before env.ts reads process.env.
  const { getDb } = await import("../server/src/db/index.js");
  const { env } = await import("../server/src/env.js");
  const { AccountingService } = await import("../server/src/services/accountingService.js");

}
```


  const db = getDb();
  console.log(
    `Database: ${
      useLocal
        ? `${env.localDbPath} (local — the service must be stopped)`
        : (process.env.SQLITE_CLOUD_URL ?? "").replace(/apikey=[^&]+/, "apikey=***")
    }`,
  );

  const balances = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const row of await db.all<{ account_code: string; net: number }>(
    `SELECT a.account_code, ROUND(SUM(jel.debit - jel.credit), 2) net
     FROM journal_entry_lines jel
     JOIN accounts a ON a.id = jel.account_id
     JOIN journal_entries je ON je.id = jel.journal_entry_id
     WHERE je.company_id = 1 AND a.account_code IN ('1200','1210')
     GROUP BY a.id`,
  )) {
    balances.set(row.account_code, Number(row.net));
  }

  const opening = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const row of await db.all<{ item_type: string; v: number }>(
    `SELECT i.item_type,
            ROUND(SUM(m.quantity * COALESCE(NULLIF((SELECT MAX(sb.purchase_price) FROM stock_batches sb
              WHERE sb.item_id = i.id AND sb.purchase_price > 0), 0), NULLIF(i.purchase_price, 0), 0)), 2) v
     FROM stock_movements m
     JOIN items i ON i.id = m.item_id
     WHERE i.company_id = 1 AND m.movement_type = 'OPENING' AND i.item_type IN (${TYPES})
     GROUP BY i.item_type`,
  )) {
    opening.set(row.item_type, Number(row.v));
  }

  const counted = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const row of await db.all<{ item_type: string; v: number }>(
    `SELECT i.item_type, ROUND(SUM(sb.quantity_in_stock * ${UNIT_COST}), 2) v
     FROM stock_batches sb
     JOIN items i ON i.id = sb.item_id
     WHERE i.company_id = 1 AND sb.quantity_in_stock > 0 AND sb.is_active = 1
       AND i.item_type IN (${TYPES})
     GROUP BY i.item_type`,
  )) {
    counted.set(row.item_type, Number(row.v));
  }

  // Per-account movement: bring each balance to its counted value, after the
  // opening value that was never booked has been added back.
  const plan = ["1200", "1210"].map((code) => {
    const type = code === "1200" ? "RAW_MATERIAL" : "PACKING_MATERIAL";
    const balance = balances.get(code) ?? 0;
    const openingValue = opening.get(type) ?? 0;
    const target = counted.get(type) ?? 0;
    return { code, type, balance, openingValue, target, delta: balance + openingValue - target };
  });

  const openingTotal = plan.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.openingValue, 0);
  const adjustTotal = plan.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.delta, 0);

  console.log("\n            balance    + opening    - counted   = adjustment");
  for (const p of plan) {
    console.log(
      `  ${p.code} ${p.type.padEnd(16)} ${p.balance.toFixed(2).padStart(12)} ${p.openingValue
        .toFixed(2)
        .padStart(12)} ${p.target.toFixed(2).padStart(12)} ${p.delta.toFixed(2).padStart(14)}`,
    );
  }
  console.log(`  opening entry total: ${openingTotal.toFixed(2)}`);
  console.log(`  COGS adjustment    : ${adjustTotal.toFixed(2)}`);

  if (openingTotal <= 0 && !plan.some((p) => Math.abs(p.delta) >= 0.01)) {
    console.log("Nothing to post.");
    return;
  }
  if (Math.abs(adjustTotal) > MAX_ADJUSTMENT && !force) {
    throw new Error(
      `Adjustment ${adjustTotal.toFixed(2)} exceeds the ${MAX_ADJUSTMENT} cap — check the stock count before posting (use --force to override).`,
    );
  }

  const accountIds = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const row of await db.all<{ id: number; account_code: string }>(
    "SELECT id, account_code FROM accounts WHERE company_id = 1 AND account_code IN ('1200','1210','3100','5000')",
  )) {
    accountIds.set(row.account_code, row.id);
  }
  const account = (code: string): number => {
    const id = accountIds.get(code);
    if (!id) throw new Error(`Account ${code} not found.`);
    return id;
  };

  const posted = new Set(
    (
      await db.all<{ reference_no: string }>(
        "SELECT reference_no FROM journal_entries WHERE company_id = 1 AND reference_no IN (?, ?)",
        [OPENING_REF, ADJUST_REF],
      )
    ).map((r) => r.reference_no),
  );

  const jobs: Array<{ ref: string; narration: string; lines: JournalLineInput[] }> = [];

  if (openingTotal > 0 && !posted.has(OPENING_REF)) {
    jobs.push({
      ref: OPENING_REF,
      narration: "Opening stock at 1 October brought into the inventory accounts",
      lines: plan
        .filter((p) => p.openingValue > 0)
        .map((p) => ({
          accountId: account(p.code),
          debit: p.openingValue,
          description: `Opening stock — ${p.type.toLowerCase().replace("_", " ")}`,
        }))
        .concat([{ accountId: account("3100"), credit: openingTotal, description: "Opening stock" }]),
    });
  }

  // A negative account delta means the books are *under* the count, so that leg
  // is a debit. Building the COGS leg from the signed total keeps the entry
  // balanced whichever way each account is off.
  if (plan.some((p) => Math.abs(p.delta) >= 0.01) && !posted.has(ADJUST_REF)) {
    const lines: JournalLineInput[] = [];
    if (Math.abs(adjustTotal) >= 0.01) {
      lines.push(
        adjustTotal > 0
          ? { accountId: account("5000"), debit: adjustTotal, description: "Materials consumed" }
          : {
              accountId: account("5000"),
              credit: -adjustTotal,
              description: "Materials consumption reversed — stock over the books",
            },
      );
    }
    for (const p of plan) {
      if (Math.abs(p.delta) < 0.01) continue;
      lines.push({
        accountId: account(p.code),
        ...(p.delta > 0 ? { credit: p.delta } : { debit: -p.delta }),
        description: `Consumed ${p.type.toLowerCase().replace("_", " ")}`,
      });
    }
    jobs.push({
      ref: ADJUST_REF,
      narration: "Raw and packing materials consumed — opening + purchases less closing stock",
      lines,
    });
  }

  if (jobs.length === 0) {
    console.log("Both entries already posted. Nothing to do.");
    return;
  }
  for (const job of jobs) console.log(`  will post ${job.ref} for ${(job.ref === OPENING_REF ? openingTotal : adjustTotal).toFixed(2)}`);

  // Cheap guard before the write: a wrong leg is easier to read here than to
  // unwind from the ledger later.
  for (const job of jobs) {
    const debit = job.lines.reduce((sum, line) => sum + (line.debit ?? 0), 0);
    const credit = job.lines.reduce((sum, line) => sum + (line.credit ?? 0), 0);
    if (Math.abs(debit - credit) >= 0.01) {
      throw new Error(
        `${job.ref} is unbalanced: debit ${debit.toFixed(2)} vs credit ${credit.toFixed(2)}.`,
      );
    }
  }

  if (!apply) {
    console.log("Dry run — nothing written. Re-run with --apply to post.");
    return;
  }

  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    const inside = new AccountingService(tx);
    for (const job of jobs) {
      const id = await inside.postJournalEntry({
        voucherType: "JOURNAL",
        entryDate,
        narration: job.narration,
        referenceNo: job.ref,
        lines: job.lines,
      });
      console.log(`Posted ${job.ref} as entry #${id} on ${entryDate}.`);
    }
  });
}

main()
  .then(async () => {
    const { closeDb } = await import("../server/src/db/index.js");
    await closeDb();
    process.exit(0);
  })
  .catch(async (err) => {
    console.error(err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);
    process.exit(1);
  });

        item_id             INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES items(id),
        batch_id            INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES stock_batches(id),
        warehouse_id        INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES warehouses(id),

                              ('PURCHASE','SALE','SALE_RETURN','PURCHASE_RETURN',

                               'EXPIRY','DAMAGE','OPENING')),
        quantity            REAL NOT NULL,
        unit_cost           REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        reference_table     TEXT,
        reference_id        INTEGER,
        movement_date       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
        notes               TEXT,
        created_by          INTEGER REFERENCES users(id)
    );

CREATE INDEX idx_movements_ref ON stock_movements(reference_table, reference_id);

  "path": "/home/oni/Documents/projects/work/BOP-software/web/scripts/fix-negative-stock.ts",
  "content": "// Clear priced negative stock batches and re-book the inventory correction.\n//\n//   npx tsx scripts/fix-negative-stock.ts --local                     # dry run\n//   npx tsx scripts/fix-negative-stock.ts --local data/erp.sqlite --apply\n//   ... --date 2026-11-19\n//\n// Four steps, one transaction:\n//   1. Reverse the old OPENING-STOCK-CORR / PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR pair (posted by\n//      post-periodic-stock-correction.ts). Those are the only manual corrections;\n//      the app's own manufacturing/reversal entries are never touched.\n//   2. Bring every *priced* negative batch to 0 and record the missing receipt\n//      as an OPENING movement. Unpriced batches are left alone — there is no\n//      value to book for them.\n//   3. Dr 1200/1210/1220 / Cr 3100 for the opening value just added.\n//   4. Dr 5000 COGS / Cr 1200 + 1210 so each raw/packing account lands on the\n//      counted batch value. Finished goods are NOT trued up here — that needs a\n//      physical count (see P0).\n//\n// Re-running is safe: every entry carries a fixed reference number and the\n// whole run is skipped once OPENING-STOCK-CORR-2 exists.\n\nimport { existsSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport type { JournalLineInput } from \"../server/src/services/accountingService.js\";\n\nconst OLD_REFS = [\"OPENING-STOCK-CORR\", \"PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR\"];\nconst REV_REFS = [\"REV-OPENING-STOCK-CORR\", \"REV-PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR\"];\nconst OPENING_REF = \"OPENING-STOCK-CORR-2\";\nconst ADJUST_REF = \"PERIODIC-STOCK-CORR-2\";\nconst MAX_ADJUSTMENT = 6_000_000;\n\nconst args = process.argv.slice(2);\nconst apply = args.includes(\"--apply\");\nconst force = args.includes(\"--force\");\nconst dateArg = args.indexOf(\"--date\");\nconst entryDate = dateArg >= 0 ? args[dateArg + 1] : new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);\n\nconst localArg = args.indexOf(\"--local\");\nconst useLocal = localArg >= 0;\nif (useLocal) {\n  process.env.ERP_DB_ENGINE = \"local\";\n  const target = args[localArg + 1];\n  if (target && !target.startsWith(\"--\")) process.env.ERP_LOCAL_DB = target;\n}\n\n/** Batch cost when it has one, else the item's purchase price. */\nconst UNIT_COST = \"COALESCE(NULLIF(sb.purchase_price,0), i.purchase_price, 0)\";\n\n/** Inventory account per item type — mirrors INVENTORY_ACCOUNT_BY_TYPE. */\nconst ACCOUNT_FOR_TYPE: Record<string, string> = {\n  RAW_MATERIAL: \"1200\",\n  PACKING_MATERIAL: \"1210\",\n  FINISHED_GOOD: \"1220\",\n};\n/** Types the periodic true-up may touch (finished goods wait for the count). */\nconst TRUE_UP_TYPES = [\"RAW_MATERIAL\", \"PACKING_MATERIAL\"];\n\ntype NegativeBatch = {\n  id: number;\n  item_id: number;\n  warehouse_id: number;\n  item_type: string;\n  batch_number: string;\n  quantity: number;\n  price: number;\n};\n\nasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n  const { getDb } = await import(\"../server/src/db/index.js\");\n  const { env } = await import(\"../server/src/env.js\");\n  const { AccountingService } = await import(\"../server/src/services/accountingService.js\");\n\n  const db = getDb();\n  console.log(\n    `Database: ${\n      useLocal ? `${env.localDbPath} (local — the service must be stopped)` : \"(cloud)\"\n    }  date: ${entryDate}`,\n  );\n\n  const existing = new Set(\n    (\n      await db.all<{ reference_no: string }>(\n        \"SELECT reference_no FROM journal_entries WHERE company_id = 1 AND reference_no IS NOT NULL\",\n      )\n    ).map((r) => r.reference_no),\n  );\n  if (existing.has(OPENING_REF)) {\n    console.log(`${OPENING_REF} already posted — nothing to do.`);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const accountIds = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const row of await db.all<{ id: number; account_code: string }>(\n    \"SELECT id, account_code FROM accounts WHERE company_id = 1 AND account_code IN ('1200','1210','1220','3100','5000')\",\n  )) {\n    accountIds.set(row.account_code, row.id);\n  }\n  const account = (code: string): number => {\n    const id = accountIds.get(code);\n    if (!id) throw new Error(`Account ${code} not found.`);\n    return id;\n  };\n\n  // --- 1. reversal of the old correction pair -------------------------------\n  type Job = { ref: string; narration: string; lines: JournalLineInput[]; total: number };\n  const jobs: Job[] = [];\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < OLD_REFS.length; i += 1) {\n    if (existing.has(REV_REFS[i])) continue;\n    const entry = await db.get<{ id: number; reference_no: string; narration: string | null }>(\n      \"SELECT id, reference_no, narration FROM journal_entries WHERE company_id = 1 AND reference_no = ?\",\n      [OLD_REFS[i]],\n    );\n    if (!entry) continue;\n    const lines = await db.all<{ account_id: number; debit: number; credit: number; description: string | null }>(\n      \"SELECT account_id, debit, credit, description FROM journal_entry_lines WHERE journal_entry_id = ? ORDER BY id\",\n      [entry.id],\n    );\n    const total = lines.reduce((sum, l) => sum + Number(l.debit), 0);\n    jobs.push({\n      ref: REV_REFS[i],\n      narration: `Reversal - ${entry.reference_no}`,\n      total,\n      lines: lines.map((l) => ({\n        accountId: l.account_id,\n        ...(Number(l.credit) > 0 ? { debit: Number(l.credit) } : { credit: Number(l.debit) }),\n        description: `Reversal - ${l.description ?? entry.reference_no}`,\n      })),\n    });\n  }\n\n  // --- 2. priced negative batches -> 0 -------------------------------------\n  const negatives = await db.all<NegativeBatch>(\n    `SELECT sb.id, sb.item_id, sb.warehouse_id, i.item_type, sb.batch_number,\n            ROUND(sb.quantity_in_stock, 4) quantity, ${UNIT_COST} price\n     FROM stock_batches sb\n     JOIN items i ON i.id = sb.item_id\n     WHERE i.company_id = 1 AND sb.quantity_in_stock < 0 AND ${UNIT_COST} > 0\n     ORDER BY sb.id`,\n  );\n  const skipped = await db.get<{ n: number }>(\n    `SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM stock_batches sb JOIN items i ON i.id = sb.item_id\n     WHERE i.company_id = 1 AND sb.quantity_in_stock < 0 AND ${UNIT_COST} <= 0`,\n  );\n\n  const openingByType = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const b of negatives) {\n    const code = ACCOUNT_FOR_TYPE[b.item_type];\n    if (!code) throw new Error(`No inventory account for item type ${b.item_type}.`);\n    openingByType.set(\n      b.item_type,\n      (openingByType.get(b.item_type) ?? 0) + Math.abs(b.quantity) * b.price,\n    );\n  }\n  const round2 = (n: number): number => Math.round(n * 100) / 100;\n  for (const [type, v] of openingByType) openingByType.set(type, round2(v));\n  const openingTotal = round2([...openingByType.values()].reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0));\n\n  // --- 3. balances and counted values (after the opening value is added) ----\n  const balances = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const row of await db.all<{ account_code: string; net: number }>(\n    `SELECT a.account_code, ROUND(SUM(jel.debit - jel.credit), 2) net\n     FROM journal_entry_lines jel\n     JOIN accounts a ON a.id = jel.account_id\n     JOIN journal_entries je ON je.id = jel.journal_entry_id\n     WHERE je.company_id = 1 AND a.account_code IN ('1200','1210','1220')\n     GROUP BY a.id`,\n  )) {\n    balances.set(row.account_code, Number(row.net));\n  }\n  const counted = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const row of await db.all<{ item_type: string; v: number }>(\n    `SELECT i.item_type, ROUND(SUM(sb.quantity_in_stock * ${UNIT_COST}), 2) v\n     FROM stock_batches sb JOIN items i ON i.id = sb.item_id\n     WHERE i.company_id = 1 AND sb.quantity_in_stock > 0 AND sb.is_active = 1\n     GROUP BY i.item_type`,\n  )) {\n    counted.set(row.item_type, Number(row.v));\n  }\n\n  // The opening entry is posted before the true-up, so its value counts as part\n  // of the balance the true-up has to move; it is also part of the batch value.\n  const plan = Object.values(ACCOUNT_FOR_TYPE).map((code) => {\n    const type = Object.keys(ACCOUNT_FOR_TYPE).find((t) => ACCOUNT_FOR_TYPE[t] === code)!;\n    const balance = balances.get(code) ?? 0;\n    const opening = openingByType.get(type) ?? 0;\n    const target = counted.get(type) ?? 0;\n    return { code, type, balance, opening, target, delta: round2(balance + opening - target) };\n  });\n\n  if (openingTotal > 0) {\n    jobs.push({\n      ref: OPENING_REF,\n      total: openingTotal,\n      narration: \"Opening stock for negative batches brought into the inventory accounts\",\n      lines: plan\n        .filter((p) => p.opening > 0)\n        .map((p) => ({\n          accountId: account(p.code),\n          debit: p.opening,\n          description: `Opening stock — ${p.type.toLowerCase().replace(\"_\", \" \")}`,\n        }))\n        .concat([{ accountId: account(\"3100\"), credit: openingTotal, description: \"Opening stock\" }]),\n    });\n  }\n\n  const tuneUp = plan.filter((p) => TRUE_UP_TYPES.includes(p.type) && Math.abs(p.delta) >= 0.01);\n  const adjustTotal = round2(tuneUp.reduce((s, p) => s + p.delta, 0));\n  if (tuneUp.length > 0) {\n    const lines: JournalLineInput[] = [\n      adjustTotal > 0\n        ? { accountId: account(\"5000\"), debit: adjustTotal, description: \"Materials consumed\" }\n        : {\n            accountId: account(\"5000\"),\n            credit: -adjustTotal,\n            description: \"Materials consumption reversed — stock over the books\",\n          },\n      ...tuneUp.map((p) => ({\n        accountId: account(p.code),\n        ...(p.delta > 0 ? { credit: p.delta } : { debit: -p.delta }),\n        description: `Consumed ${p.type.toLowerCase().replace(\"_\", \" \")}`,\n      })),\n    ];\n    jobs.push({\n      ref: ADJUST_REF,\n      total: adjustTotal,\n      narration: \"Raw and packing materials consumed — opening + purchases less closing stock\",\n      lines,\n    });\n  }\n\n  // --- report ---------------------------------------------------------------\n  console.log(`\\n  priced negative batches -> 0 : ${negatives.length}`);\n  console.log(`  unpriced negative batches   : ${skipped?.n ?? 0} (left alone, no value to book)`);\n  console.log(\"\\n            balance    + opening    - counted   = adjustment\");\n  for (const p of plan) {\n    console.log(\n      `  ${p.code} ${p.type.padEnd(16)} ${p.balance.toFixed(2).padStart(12)} ${p.opening\n        .toFixed(2)\n        .padStart(12)} ${p.target.toFixed(2).padStart(12)} ${p.delta.toFixed(2).padStart(14)}${\n        TRUE_UP_TYPES.includes(p.type) ? \"\" : \"   (not trued up)\"\n      }`,\n    );\n  }\n  if (jobs.length === 0) {\n    console.log(\"\\nNothing to post.\");\n    return;\n  }\n  for (const job of jobs) console.log(`  will post ${job.ref} for ${job.total.toFixed(2)}`);\n\n  if (Math.abs(adjustTotal) > MAX_ADJUSTMENT && !force) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `Adjustment ${adjustTotal.toFixed(2)} exceeds the ${MAX_ADJUSTMENT} cap — check the count first (use --force to override).`,\n    );\n  }\n  for (const job of jobs) {\n    const debit = round2(job.lines.reduce((s, l) => s + Number(l.debit ?? 0), 0));\n    const credit = round2(job.lines.reduce((s, l) => s + Number(l.credit ?? 0), 0));\n    if (debit !== credit) {\n      throw new Error(`${job.ref} is unbalanced: debit ${debit} vs credit ${credit}.`);\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!apply) {\n    console.log(\"\\nDry run — nothing written. Re-run with --apply to post.\");\n    return;\n  }\n\n  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    const inside = new AccountingService(tx);\n\n    // Reverse first, so the true-up below sees the post-reversal balances.\n    for (const job of jobs.filter((j) => REV_REFS.includes(j.ref))) {\n      const id = await inside.postJournalEntry({\n        voucherType: \"JOURNAL\",\n        entryDate,\n        narration: job.narration,\n        referenceNo: job.ref,\n        lines: job.lines,\n      });\n      // Same marker reverseManualJournal uses, so the UI cannot reverse it twice.\n      const original = OLD_REFS[REV_REFS.indexOf(job.ref)];\n      await tx.run(\n        \"UPDATE journal_entries SET source_table = 'journal_entries', source_id = ? WHERE company_id = 1 AND reference_no = ?\",\n        [id, original],\n      );\n      console.log(`Posted ${job.ref} as entry #${id} on ${entryDate}.`);\n    }\n\n    for (const b of negatives) {\n      await tx.run(\"UPDATE stock_batches SET quantity_in_stock = 0 WHERE id = ?\", [b.id]);\n      await tx.run(\n        `INSERT INTO stock_movements\n           (item_id, batch_id, warehouse_id, movement_type, quantity, unit_cost,\n            reference_table, movement_date, notes)\n         VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'OPENING', ?, ?, 'negative_stock_fix', ?, ?)`,\n        [\n          b.item_id,\n          b.id,\n          b.warehouse_id,\n          Math.abs(b.quantity),\n          b.price,\n          entryDate,\n          `Missing receipt for negative batch ${b.batch_number}`,\n        ],\n      );\n    }\n    console.log(`Cleared ${negatives.length} priced negative batches and recorded their OPENING movements.`);\n\n    for (const job of jobs.filter((j) => !REV_REFS.includes(j.ref))) {\n      const id = await inside.postJournalEntry({\n        voucherType: \"JOURNAL\",\n        entryDate,\n        narration: job.narration,\n        referenceNo: job.ref,\n        lines: job.lines,\n      });\n      console.log(`Posted ${job.ref} as entry #${id} on ${entryDate}.`);\n    }\n  });\n}\n\nmain()\n  .then(async () => {\n    const { closeDb } = await import(\"../server/src/db/index.js\");\n    await closeDb();\n    process.exit(0);\n  })\n  .catch(async (err) => {\n    console.error(err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);\n    process.exit(1);\n  });\n"
}
```

/** The database abstraction every repository depends on. */
export type SqlValue = string | number | bigint | Uint8Array | null;
export type Row = Record<string, unknown>;
export interface RunResult {
  changes: number;
  lastInsertRowid: number;
}
export interface SqlDatabase {
  /** Run a SELECT and return every row. */
  all<T = Row>(sql: string, params?: readonly SqlValue[]): Promise<T[]>;
  /** Run a SELECT and return the first row (or undefined). */
  get<T = Row>(sql: string, params?: readonly SqlValue[]): Promise<T | undefined>;
  /** Run a single write statement. */
  run(sql: string, params?: readonly SqlValue[]): Promise<RunResult>;
  /** Run one or more statements with no parameters (DDL, migrations). */
  exec(sql: string): Promise<void>;
  /**
   * Run `fn` inside a database transaction. Nested calls join the outer
   * transaction so a service calling another service commits atomically.
   */
  transaction<T>(fn: (tx: SqlDatabase) => Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
  close(): Promise<void>;
  /** Best-effort cancellation of a long-running statement. */
  interrupt(): Promise<void>;
  /** Whether the underlying connection is currently live. */
  isConnected(): Promise<boolean>;
  /** Real query-based health probe; false on transient faults. */
  ping(): Promise<boolean>;
}

  "newString": "  // --- 3. balances and counted values (after the opening value is added) ----\n  const balances = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const row of await db.all<{ account_code: string; net: number }>(\n    `SELECT a.account_code, ROUND(SUM(jel.debit - jel.credit), 2) net\n     FROM journal_entry_lines jel\n     JOIN accounts a ON a.id = jel.account_id\n     JOIN journal_entries je ON je.id = jel.journal_entry_id\n     WHERE je.company_id = 1 AND a.account_code IN ('1200','1210','1220')\n     GROUP BY a.id`,\n  )) {\n    balances.set(row.account_code, Number(row.net));\n  }\n  // The true-up must land on the balances *after* the reversal. The reversal is\n  // posted inside the same transaction, so subtract the not-yet-reversed old\n  // correction here (a re-run that already reversed it skips this).\n  for (let i = 0; i < OLD_REFS.length; i += 1) {\n    if (existing.has(REV_REFS[i])) continue;\n    for (const row of await db.all<{ account_code: string; net: number }>(\n      `SELECT a.account_code, ROUND(SUM(jel.debit - jel.credit), 2) net\n       FROM journal_entry_lines jel\n       JOIN accounts a ON a.id = jel.account_id\n       JOIN journal_entries je ON je.id = jel.journal_entry_id\n       WHERE je.company_id = 1 AND je.reference_no = ? AND a.account_code IN ('1200','1210','1220')\n       GROUP BY a.id`,\n      [OLD_REFS[i]],\n    )) {\n      balances.set(row.account_code, round2((balances.get(row.account_code) ?? 0) - Number(row.net)));\n    }\n  }"
}

200:     if (inventoryRows.length === 0) return;
201: 
202:     const values = await this.inventoryTagValues(to);

221:         });
