262:   /** Items held in the store whose every batch has no cost, so they value at zero. */
263:   private async unpricedStockItems(
264:     itemType: ItemType,
265:   ): Promise<Array<{ name: string; quantity: number }>> {
266:     return this.db.all<{ name: string; quantity: number }>(
267:       `SELECT i.item_name AS name, SUM(sb.quantity_in_stock) AS quantity
268:          FROM stock_batches sb
269:          JOIN items i ON i.id = sb.item_id
270:         WHERE sb.is_active = 1 AND i.is_active = 1 AND i.company_id = ?
271:           AND i.item_type = ?
272:           AND sb.quantity_in_stock > 0
273:           AND sb.purchase_price = 0
274:           AND (sb.raw_unit_cost + sb.packing_unit_cost) = 0
275:         GROUP BY i.id, i.item_name
276:         ORDER BY quantity DESC`,
277:       [this.companyId, itemType],
278:     );
279:   }
280: 
281:   /**
282:    * Inputs pulled into production and packing runs that carry no cost anywhere:
283:    * no purchase price and no costed batch. Their value never reached the
284:    * finished goods made from them.
285:    */
286:   private async unpricedProductionInputs(): Promise<Array<{ name: string; quantity: number }>> {
287:     return this.db.all<{ name: string; quantity: number }>(
288:       `WITH runs AS (
289:          SELECT bom_id, actual_quantity FROM production_orders WHERE actual_quantity > 0
290:          UNION ALL
291:          SELECT bom_id, actual_quantity FROM packing_orders WHERE actual_quantity > 0
292:        )
293:        SELECT i.item_name AS name,
294:               SUM(bc.quantity_required * r.actual_quantity / NULLIF(b.output_quantity, 0)) AS quantity
295:          FROM runs r
296:          JOIN bill_of_materials b ON b.id = r.bom_id
297:          JOIN bom_components bc ON bc.bom_id = b.id
298:          JOIN items i ON i.id = bc.component_item_id
299:         WHERE i.company_id = ?
300:           AND NOT EXISTS (
301:                 SELECT 1 FROM purchase_invoice_items p
302:                  WHERE p.item_id = i.id AND p.unit_cost > 0)
303:           AND NOT EXISTS (
304:                 SELECT 1 FROM stock_batches sb
305:                  WHERE sb.item_id = i.id
306:                    AND (sb.purchase_price > 0 OR (sb.raw_unit_cost + sb.packing_unit_cost) > 0))
307:         GROUP BY i.id, i.item_name
308:         ORDER BY quantity DESC`,
309:       [this.companyId],
310:     );
311:   }
312: 
313:   /**
314:    * Material taken out of an inventory account by manufacturing production, as
315:    * at `to`. Stock has no issue movements in this dataset, so the manufacturing
316:    * vouchers are the only record of what was consumed.
317:    */
318:   private async issuedToProduction(accountId: number, to: string | null): Promise<number> {
319:     const row = await this.db.get<{ value: number }>(
320:       `SELECT COALESCE(SUM(l.credit), 0) AS value
321:          FROM journal_entry_lines l
322:          JOIN journal_entries je ON je.id = l.journal_entry_id
323:         WHERE l.account_id = ? AND je.voucher_type = 'MANUFACTURING'
324:           ${to ? "AND date(je.entry_date) <= date(?)" : ""}`,
325:       to ? [accountId, to] : [accountId],
326:     );
327:     return toStorage(row?.value ?? 0);
328:   }
329: 
330:   /**
331:    * Cost of every finished good sold as at `to`, taken from the invoices: each
332:    * sales-invoice line contributes its own quantity at that item's average
333:    * intake cost (purchases, production runs and opening stock). Quantities come
334:    * from the invoice, so sales posted without a stock movement are still
335:    * counted; only the cost has to be looked up.
336:    */
337:   private async soldFinishedGoods(to: string | null): Promise<number> {
338:     const row = await this.db.get<{ value: number }>(
339:       `WITH intake AS (
340:          SELECT m.item_id,
341:                 SUM(m.quantity * m.unit_cost) / NULLIF(SUM(m.quantity), 0) AS unit_cost
342:            FROM stock_movements m
343:           WHERE m.movement_type IN ('PURCHASE','PRODUCTION_IN','OPENING')
344:             AND m.quantity > 0 AND m.unit_cost > 0
345:             ${to ? "AND date(m.movement_date) <= date(?)" : ""}
346:           GROUP BY m.item_id
347:        )
348:        SELECT COALESCE(SUM(sii.quantity * COALESCE(cost.unit_cost, 0)), 0) AS value
349:          FROM sales_invoice_items sii
350:          JOIN sales_invoices si ON si.id = sii.invoice_id
351:          JOIN items it ON it.id = sii.item_id
352:          LEFT JOIN intake cost ON cost.item_id = sii.item_id
353:         WHERE it.item_type = 'FINISHED_GOOD' AND it.company_id = ?
354:           ${to ? "AND date(si.invoice_date) <= date(?)" : ""}`,
355:       to ? [to, this.companyId, to] : [this.companyId],
356:     );
357:     return toStorage(row?.value ?? 0);
358:   }
359: 
360:   /** Stock value by item type and tag, as at `to` (null = current stock). */
361:   private async inventoryTagValues(
362:     to: string | null,
363:   ): Promise<Array<{ item_type: string; tag: string | null; value: number }>> {
364:     if (to === null) {
365:       return this.db.all(
366:         `SELECT i.item_type, t.name AS tag,
367:                 SUM(sb.quantity_in_stock *
368:                     CASE WHEN (sb.raw_unit_cost + sb.packing_unit_cost) > 0
369:                          THEN sb.raw_unit_cost + sb.packing_unit_cost
370:                          ELSE sb.purchase_price END) AS value
371:            FROM stock_batches sb
372:            JOIN items i ON i.id = sb.item_id
373:            LEFT JOIN item_tag_links l ON l.item_id = i.id
374:            LEFT JOIN item_tags t ON t.id = l.tag_id
375:           WHERE sb.is_active = 1 AND i.is_active = 1 AND i.company_id = ?
376:             AND sb.quantity_in_stock > 0
377:           GROUP BY i.item_type, t.id`,
378:         [this.companyId],
379:       );
380:     }
381: 
382:     return this.db.all(
383:       `WITH latest AS (
384:          SELECT h.item_id, h.tag_id, h.action,
385:                 ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY h.item_id, h.tag_id
386:                                    ORDER BY h.changed_at DESC, h.id DESC) AS rn
387:            FROM item_tag_history h
388:           WHERE date(h.changed_at) <= date(?)

390:        membership AS (
391:          SELECT item_id, tag_id FROM latest WHERE rn = 1 AND action = 'ADD'

393:        qty AS (

395:                 CASE
396:                   WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM stock_movements m0 WHERE m0.batch_id = sb.id)

398:                                             FROM stock_movements m
399:                                            WHERE m.batch_id = sb.id
400:                                              AND date(m.movement_date) <= date(?)), 0))
401:                   WHEN date(sb.received_date) <= date(?) THEN MAX(0, sb.quantity_in_stock)
402:                   ELSE 0
403:                 END AS quantity,
404:                 CASE WHEN (sb.raw_unit_cost + sb.packing_unit_cost) > 0
405:                      THEN sb.raw_unit_cost + sb.packing_unit_cost
406:                      ELSE sb.purchase_price END AS unit_cost
407:            FROM stock_batches sb
408:           WHERE sb.is_active = 1
409:        )
410:        SELECT i.item_type, t.name AS tag, SUM(q.quantity * q.unit_cost) AS value
411:          FROM qty q

413:          LEFT JOIN membership m ON m.item_id = i.id
414:          LEFT JOIN item_tags t ON t.id = m.tag_id
415:         WHERE i.is_active = 1 AND i.company_id = ?

            </td>

            <td className="num">{amount(row.closingDebit)}</td>
            <td className="num">{amount(row.closingCredit)}</td>
          </tr>
          {(() => {
            const tags = row.tagBreakdown ?? [];
            // A dated report carries no tag breakdown: there is no shelf in the
            // past, so the stock lines are simply absent.
            const hasStock = tags.length > 0;
            const sold = row.soldValue;
            const used = row.usedValue;
            const stockTotal = tags.reduce((total, tag) => total + tag.value, 0);
            // For finished goods the account is sold + on the shelf, so the gap
            // is the stock value against what is left after the sold part.
            const expectedInStock = sold == null ? row.closingDebit : row.closingDebit - sold;
            const difference = stockTotal - expectedInStock;
            if (tags.length === 0 && sold == null && used == null) return null;
            const indent = { paddingLeft: 28 + row.depth * 12, fontSize: 12 };
            // A line inside the memo block, with free text on the right.
            const detailRow = (key: string, label: string, note: string, level: number) => (
              <tr key={key} style={{ opacity: 0.6 }}>
                <td />
                <td
                  style={{
                    ...indent,
                    paddingLeft: indent.paddingLeft + 16 * level,
                    fontSize: 11,
                  }}
                >
                  {label}
                </td>
                <td className="num" />
                <td className="num" />
                <td className="num" />
                <td className="num" />
                <td className="num" style={{ fontSize: 11 }}>
                  {note}
                </td>
                <td className="num" />
              </tr>
            );
            // Memo lines sit under the account and never feed the report totals.
            // `nested` tucks a line under the memo line above it, e.g. a tag
            // breaking down the "In stock" total.
            const memoRow = (
              key: string,
              label: string,
              value: number,
              strong = false,
              nested = false,
            ) => (
              <tr key={key} style={{ opacity: 0.75 }}>
                <td />
                <td style={nested ? { ...indent, paddingLeft: indent.paddingLeft + 16 } : indent}>
                  {nested ? "·" : "↳"} {label}
                </td>
                <td className="num" />
                <td className="num" />
                <td className="num" />
                <td className="num" />
                <td className="num">{strong ? money(value) : amount(value)}</td>
                <td className="num" />
              </tr>
            );
            return (
              <>
                {/* Finished goods: what the invoices sold, then what is left. */}
                {sold != null ? (
                  <>
                    {memoRow(`${row.accountId}-sold`, "Sold FG (per invoices)", sold, true)}
                    {/* No shelf on a dated report, so no stock line. */}
                    {showStock && hasStock &&
                      memoRow(`${row.accountId}-stock`, "FG in stock", stockTotal, true)}
                  </>
                ) : used != null ? (
                  <>
                    {memoRow(`${row.accountId}-used`, "Used in production", used, true)}
                    {row.consumedValue != null &&
                      memoRow(
                        `${row.accountId}-consumed`,
                        "Consumed (opening + purchases − stock)",
                        row.consumedValue,
                        true,
                      )}
                    {showStock && hasStock && memoRow(`${row.accountId}-stock`, "In stock", stockTotal, true)}
                    {tags.map((tag) =>
                      memoRow(`${row.accountId}-tag-${tag.name}`, tag.name, tag.value, false, true),
                    )}
                  </>
                ) : (
                  tags.map((tag) =>
                    memoRow(`${row.accountId}-tag-${tag.name}`, tag.name, tag.value, false, true),
                  )
                )}
                {showStock && hasStock &&
                  memoRow(`${row.accountId}-difference`, "Difference (stock - GL)", difference, true)}
                {/* Opt-in: what is behind the figure but carries no cost. */}
                {showUnpriced && (row.unpricedItems?.length ?? 0) > 0
                  ? [
                      detailRow(
                        `${row.accountId}-unpriced`,
                        `Unpriced items (${row.unpricedItems!.length})`,
                        "",
                        1,
                      ),
                      ...row.unpricedItems!.map((item) =>
                        detailRow(
                          `${row.accountId}-unpriced-${item.name}`,
                          item.name,
                          amount(item.quantity),
                          2,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ]
                  : null}
              </>
            );
          })()}
        </Fragment>
      ))}

  async balanceSheet(asAt?: string | null): Promise<BalanceSheetResult> {
    const rows = await this.accounting.getTrialBalance(this.companyId, { to: asAt ?? null });
    return this.balanceSheetFrom(rows);
  }

  /** Position as at a date, from rows another report call already fetched. */
  balanceSheetFrom(rows: readonly TrialBalanceRow[]): BalanceSheetResult {
    let assets = new Decimal(0);
    let liabilities = new Decimal(0);
    let equity = new Decimal(0);
    let netProfit = new Decimal(0);
    for (const row of rows) {
      const debit = new Decimal(row.closingDebit);
      const credit = new Decimal(row.closingCredit);
      switch (row.accountType) {
        case "ASSET":
          assets = assets.plus(debit).minus(credit);
          break;
        case "LIABILITY":
          liabilities = liabilities.plus(credit).minus(debit);
          break;
        case "EQUITY":
          equity = equity.plus(credit).minus(debit);
          break;
        case "REVENUE":
          netProfit = netProfit.plus(credit).minus(debit);
          break;
        case "EXPENSE":
          netProfit = netProfit.minus(debit).plus(credit);
          break;
      }
    }
    const equityWithProfit = equity.plus(netProfit);
    return {
      assets: toStorage(assets),
      liabilities: toStorage(liabilities),
      equity: toStorage(equityWithProfit),
      netProfit: toStorage(netProfit),
      balanced: assets
        .minus(liabilities)
        .minus(equityWithProfit)
        .abs()
        .lessThan(new Decimal("0.01")),
    };
  }

  /**
   * Materials consumed on the old software's periodic basis:
   * `opening + purchases − closing`.
   *
   * Without `to` the closing figure is the physical count (today's stock), so a
   * wrong BOM or an unpriced item cannot move the number — only a wrong count
   * can. With `to` the closing figure is the inventory account's balance at that
   * date, because a past count does not exist; the answer is then what the books
   * say was consumed in the period.

## Assistant

## Where FG appears (and what each place actually shows)

Live figures from the server right now:

| Source | FG value shown | Formula |
|---|---|---|
| Trial Balance memo "FG in stock" | **457,251.12** (32 batches) | `qty × (raw+packing unit cost, else purchase_price)` on positive active batches |
| Trial Balance memo "Sold FG (per invoices)" | **11,536,372.06** | invoice qty × item's average intake cost (PURCHASE / PRODUCTION_IN / OPENING movements) |
| Trial Balance GL column (1220) | **4,120,845.02** | GL balance |
| Balance Sheet | **4,120,845.02** inside total assets | GL 1220 closing only — no count, no memo |
| P&L | nothing | FG only reaches P&L via COGS 5000/5001/5002 |
| Materials Consumption page | nothing | filtered to raw + packing only |
| Dashboard "Inventory" | qty × `purchase_price` | falls back to GL 1200+1210+1220 only if no batch has a cost |

## What the trial balance puts under account 1220

Four memo lines, all nested under the account row, **never added to report totals**:

1. **Sold FG (per invoices)** — 11,536,372.06
2. **FG in stock** — 457,251.12 (only on an *undated* report; a dated report shows no shelf, so this line disappears)
3. **Per-tag breakdown** under "FG in stock" — stock value split by item tag
4. **Difference (stock − GL)** — computed as `stockTotal − (closingDebit − sold)` = **+7,872,778.16** today
5. Optional, only with the "show unpriced" toggle: **Unpriced items (49)** — components used in production/packing runs that have no purchase price and no priced batch, listed by name + quantity

## Three things worth knowing

- **Negative batches are invisible in every report.** Every stock query filters `quantity_in_stock > 0`. The 4 remaining negative FG batches (`(Unpacked) MAGNET`, `ES 200`, `PHYTO FAT`) contribute nothing to "FG in stock" — they just vanish. They're only visible on the Items/batches list.
- **"Sold FG" is a memo, not the COGS that was posted.** It values every sold FG line at the item's average intake cost. That's why it reads 11.54M while 1220 holds only 4.12M — the gap is the FG relief you haven't posted.
- **Two different stock formulas exist.** Reports use `raw+packing unit cost, else purchase_price`; the dashboard uses `purchase_price` only. They agree today for FG (457,251.12 both), but will diverge as soon as produced batches get a `raw_unit_cost`.

So for your P0: the report already tells you the size of the gap — the "Difference" line at **+7.87M** is stock-vs-GL after removing sold goods. The FG true-up you post should make GL 1220 land on the 457,251.12 shelf value.

*Skipped: I didn't check the XLSX/PDF exporters' FG memo layout beyond confirming they use the same fields, or the Items page batch columns.*

## Assistant

## FG sold through sales invoices — the actual numbers

| | Oct 2026 | Nov 2026 | Total |
|---|---|---|---|
| Invoices | 99 | 38 | 137 |
| Lines | — | — | 332 |
| Quantity | 32,722 | 7,419 | **40,141** |
| Invoiced (selling value) | 29,076,546.00 | 14,830,078.00 | **43,906,624.00** |
| Cost of those units | 5,749,781.63 | 5,786,590.43 | **11,536,372.06** |

That cost figure is exactly the **"Sold FG (per invoices)"** memo line on the trial balance under 1220. It is computed from the invoice lines themselves — invoice quantity × the item's average intake cost (`PURCHASE` / `PRODUCTION_IN` / `OPENING` movements) — so it does **not** depend on the 1220 GL balance or on any stock count.

## Two gaps that understate it

**1. 13 FG items were sold with no cost at all** — 3,954 units, 3,421,900 invoiced, cost recorded as 0:

| Item | Qty | Invoiced |
|---|---|---|
| PHYTO FAT 25 KG | 94 | 1,409,700 |
| FEBRO MEON 120 ML | 1,728 | 518,400 |
| immune forte oral liquid | 120 | 264,000 |
| HEAATIC OPT FORT 25 KG | 40 | 220,000 |
| Viro-immune 5 lit | 20 | 200,000 |
| RespiGuard Liquid 1 lit | 60 | 155,760 |
| BIO ESEL 200 Liquid 5 lit | 8 | 110,880 |
| TIMPO EX 120 ML | 1,778 | 110,160 |
| BOP Copper oral Liquid 5 lit | 40 | 108,000 |
| Broncokill liquid 5 lit | 20 | 100,000 |
| UDDER BOOST 25 KG | 6 | 90,000 |
| Hepatic-Optimizer Powder | 10 | 75,000 |
| Toxi gold forte oral powder | 30 | 60,000 |

**2. Posted COGS ≠ invoiced cost.** GL 5000 = **10,314,976.86** vs **11,536,372.06** from the invoices → **1,221,395.20 short**.

## So what this changes

If FG sold is all you care about, the 1220 shelf value (457,251.12) and the 3.66M "overstatement" stop mattering — they only exist to make the balance sheet tie. The two things that matter are:

1. **Dr 5000 / Cr 1220 for 1,221,395.20** so GL COGS equals the cost of what was actually invoiced.
2. **Price the 13 items above** — otherwise their cost stays 0 and COGS is still understated by whatever those 3,954 units really cost.

Point 2 is the blocker: the 1,221,395.20 assumes the 13 zero-cost items are worth nothing. They're not.

*Skipped: I didn't check whether those 13 have purchase invoices at a price (only that no priced stock movement exists), so their true cost is unknown from the DB.*

Which do you want me to do — post the 1.22M COGS alignment now, or first find the real cost of those 13 items?

  "newString": "    for (const row of inventoryRows) {\n      const itemType = INVENTORY_ITEM_TYPE_BY_CODE[row.accountCode];\n      // Finished goods show only what the invoices sold — no tag split, no count.\n      if (!itemType || itemType === \"FINISHED_GOOD\") continue;\n      const perTag = byType.get(itemType);\n      if (!perTag) continue;\n      row.tagBreakdown = [...perTag.entries()]\n        .map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value }))\n        .sort((a, b) => {\n          if (a.name === \"Untagged\") return 1;\n          if (b.name === \"Untagged\") return -1;\n          return a.name.localeCompare(b.name, undefined, { sensitivity: \"base\" });\n        });\n    }\n\n    // Finished goods report one figure: what the sales invoices sold. It is\n    // built from the invoices themselves, so a sale that never relieved stock\n    // still counts.\n    const fgRow = inventoryRows.find(\n      (row) => INVENTORY_ITEM_TYPE_BY_CODE[row.accountCode] === \"FINISHED_GOOD\",\n    );\n    if (fgRow) {\n      const sold = await this.soldFinishedGoods(to);\n      fgRow.soldValue = sold.total;\n      fgRow.soldItems = sold.items;\n    }",

{
  "newString": "  /**\n   * Value of every finished good sold as at `to`, taken from the invoices:\n   * each sales-invoice line contributes its own quantity and line total. That\n   * is what the goods were billed at, so it does not depend on the posting\n   * code having relieved stock or on how the batches are costed.\n   */\n  private async soldFinishedGoods(\n    to: string | null,\n  ): Promise<{ total: number; items: Array<{ name: string; quantity: number; value: number }> }> {\n    const rows = await this.db.all<{ name: string; quantity: number; value: number }>(\n      `SELECT it.item_name AS name,\n              SUM(sii.quantity) AS quantity,\n              SUM(sii.line_total) AS value\n         FROM sales_invoice_items sii\n         JOIN sales_invoices si ON si.id = sii.invoice_id\n         JOIN items it ON it.id = sii.item_id\n        WHERE it.item_type = 'FINISHED_GOOD' AND it.company_id = ?\n          ${to ? \"AND date(si.invoice_date) <= date(?)\" : \"\"}\n        GROUP BY it.id, it.item_name\n        ORDER BY value DESC`,\n      to ? [this.companyId, to] : [this.companyId],\n    );\n    const items = rows.map((row) => ({\n      name: row.name,\n      quantity: toStorage(row.quantity),\n      value: toStorage(row.value),\n    }));\n    return { items, total: toStorage(items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.value, 0)) };\n  }",
  "oldString": "  /**\n   * Cost of every finished good sold as at `to`, taken from the invoices: each\n   * sales-invoice line contributes its own quantity at that item's average\n   * intake cost (purchases, production runs and opening stock). Quantities come\n   * from the invoice, so sales posted without a stock movement are still\n   * counted; only the cost has to be looked up.\n   */\n  private async soldFinishedGoods(to: string | null): Promise<number> {\n    const row = await this.db.get<{ value: number }>(\n      `WITH intake AS (\n         SELECT m.item_id,\n                SUM(m.quantity * m.unit_cost) / NULLIF(SUM(m.quantity), 0) AS unit_cost\n           FROM stock_movements m\n          WHERE m.movement_type IN ('PURCHASE','PRODUCTION_IN','OPENING')\n            AND m.quantity > 0 AND m.unit_cost > 0\n            ${to ? \"AND date(m.movement_date) <= date(?)\" : \"\"}\n          GROUP BY m.item_id\n       )\n       SELECT COALESCE(SUM(sii.quantity * COALESCE(cost.unit_cost, 0)), 0) AS value\n         FROM sales_invoice_items sii\n         JOIN sales_invoices si ON si.id = sii.invoice_id\n         JOIN items it ON it.id = sii.item_id\n         LEFT JOIN intake cost ON cost.item_id = sii.item_id\n        WHERE it.item_type = 'FINISHED_GOOD' AND it.company_id = ?\n          ${to ? \"AND date(si.invoice_date) <= date(?)\" : \"\"}`,\n      to ? [to, this.companyId, to] : [this.companyId],\n    );\n    return toStorage(row?.value ?? 0);\n  }",

}

Their value never reached the\n   * finished goods made from them.\n   */\n  private async unpricedProductionInputs(): Promise<Array<{ name: string; quantity: number }>> {\n    return this.db.all<{ name: string; quantity: number }>(\n      `WITH runs AS (\n         SELECT bom_id, actual_quantity FROM production_orders WHERE actual_quantity > 0\n         UNION ALL\n         SELECT bom_id, actual_quantity FROM packing_orders WHERE actual_quantity > 0\n       )\n       SELECT i.item_name AS name,\n              SUM(bc.quantity_required * r.actual_quantity / NULLIF(b.output_quantity, 0)) AS quantity\n         FROM runs r\n         JOIN bill_of_materials b ON b.id = r.bom_id\n         JOIN bom_components bc ON bc.bom_id = b.id\n         JOIN items i ON i.id = bc.component_item_id\n        WHERE i.company_id = ?\n          AND NOT EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM purchase_invoice_items p\n                 WHERE p.item_id = i.id AND p.unit_cost > 0)\n          AND NOT EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM stock_batches sb\n                 WHERE sb.item_id = i.id\n                   AND (sb.purchase_price > 0 OR (sb.raw_unit_cost + sb.packing_unit_cost) > 0))\n        GROUP BY i.id, i.item_name\n        ORDER BY quantity DESC`,\n      [this.companyId],\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Material taken out of an inventory account by manufacturing production, as\n   * at `to`. Stock has no issue movements in this dataset, so the manufacturing\n   * vouchers are the only record of what was consumed.\n   */",

import { LocalSqliteDatabase } from "../../src/db/localSqlite.js";
import { seedDemoData, type DemoIds } from "../../src/seedData.js";
import type { SqlDatabase } from "../../src/db/types.js";

export interface TestContext {
  db: SqlDatabase;
  ids: DemoIds;
}

/** Fresh in-memory SQLite database with schema, seed accounts and demo data. */
export async function freshDb(): Promise<TestContext> {
  const db = new LocalSqliteDatabase(":memory:");
  const ids = await seedDemoData(db);
  return { db, ids };
}

export async function closeDb(db: SqlDatabase): Promise<void> {
  await db.close();
}

/** Look up an account id by code (company 1). */
export async function accountId(db: SqlDatabase, code: string): Promise<number> {
  const row = await db.get<{ id: number }>(
    "SELECT id FROM accounts WHERE account_code = ? AND company_id = 1",
    [code],
  );
  if (!row) throw new Error(`Account ${code} not seeded`);
  return row.id;
}

  "newString": "  it(\"breaks inventory accounts down by tag, replaying membership as at the report end\", async () => {\n    const items = new ItemService(ctx.db);\n    const raw = await items.create({\n      itemName: \"Raw Tagged\",\n      unit: \"KG\",\n      itemType: \"RAW_MATERIAL\",\n    });\n    const opening = await items.addOpeningStock({ itemId: raw.id, quantity: 10, unitCost: 50 });\n    // Backdate the receipt so a historical report sees the stock it carried then.\n    await ctx.db.run(\"UPDATE stock_movements SET movement_date = '2024-01-01' WHERE batch_id = ?\", [\n      opening.batchId,\n    ]);\n    const tag = await items.createTag(\"bottle\");\n    await items.setTagItems(tag.id, { add: [raw.id] });\n\n    const current = await reports.trialBalance();\n    expect(current.rows.find((row) => row.accountCode === \"1200\")?.tagBreakdown).toEqual([\n      { name: \"bottle\", value: 500 },\n    ]);\n\n    // Before the assignment the same stock is untagged.\n    const before = await reports.trialBalance({ to: \"2024-06-30\" });\n    expect(before.rows.find((row) => row.accountCode === \"1200\")?.tagBreakdown).toEqual([\n      { name: \"Untagged\", value: 500 },\n    ]);\n\n    // Backdate the assignment: the report now sees the tag on that date.\n    await ctx.db.run(\n      \"UPDATE item_tag_history SET changed_at = '2024-01-01 00:00:00' WHERE tag_id = ?\",\n      [tag.id],\n    );\n    const backdated = await reports.trialBalance({ to: \"2024-06-30\" });\n    expect(backdated.rows.find((row) => row.accountCode === \"1200\")?.tagBreakdown).toEqual([\n      { name: \"bottle\", value: 500 },\n    ]);\n  });",

fi
AFTER=$(git -C "$REPO_DIR" rev-parse HEAD 2>/dev/null || echo "")
CHANGED_FILES=""
if [ -n "$BEFORE" ] && [ -n "$AFTER" ] && [ "$BEFORE" != "$AFTER" ]; then
    CHANGED_FILES=$(git -C "$REPO_DIR" diff --name-only "$BEFORE" "$AFTER")
    log "updated $BEFORE -> $AFTER"
fi

# --- 2. dependencies -------------------------------------------------------
NEED_INSTALL=0
[ -d "$WEB_DIR/node_modules" ] || NEED_INSTALL=1
if printf '%s' "$CHANGED_FILES" | grep -q "package-lock.json"; then NEED_INSTALL=1; fi
if [ "$NEED_INSTALL" = 1 ]; then
    log "installing dependencies"
    ( cd "$WEB_DIR" && (npm ci --no-audit --no-fund || npm install --no-audit --no-fund) ) || \
        log "package install FAILED - continuing with what is installed"
fi

# --- 3. build --------------------------------------------------------------
NEED_BUILD=0
[ -n "$CHANGED_FILES" ] && NEED_BUILD=1
[ -f "$WEB_DIR/server/dist/index.js" ]    || NEED_BUILD=1
[ -f "$WEB_DIR/client/dist/index.html" ]  || NEED_BUILD=1
BUILD_OK=1
if [ "$NEED_BUILD" = 1 ]; then
    log "building"
    ( cd "$WEB_DIR" && npm run build ) || { BUILD_OK=0; log "BUILD FAILED"; }
else
    log "code unchanged - skipping build"
fi
if [ "$BUILD_OK" = 0 ] && [ "$BOOT" = 0 ]; then
    log "not restarting: a failed build must not replace a working one"
    exit 1
fi

# --- 4. tests --------------------------------------------------------------
if [ "$SKIP_TESTS" = 0 ] && [ "$BUILD_OK" = 1 ]; then
    log "running tests"
    ( cd "$WEB_DIR" && npm test ) || { log "TESTS FAILED - not deploying"; exit 1; }
fi

  private inventoryMemoCsv(
    row: TrialBalanceRow,
    label: (text: string) => string,
  ): string[] {
    const tags = row.tagBreakdown ?? [];
    if (tags.length === 0) return [];
    const stock = tags.reduce((total, tag) => total.plus(tag.value), new Decimal(0));
    const line = (name: string, value: number): string =>
      ["", label(name), "", "", "", "", "", value.toFixed(2), ""].join(",");
    return [
      ...tags.map((tag) => line(`↳ ${tag.name}`, tag.value)),
      line("Difference (stock - GL)", toStorage(stock.minus(row.closingDebit))),
    ];
  }

  /** CSV lines for the "Unpriced items" breakdown under an inventory account. */
  private unpricedCsvRows(
    row: TrialBalanceRow,
    label: (text: string) => string,
  ): string[] {
    const items = row.unpricedItems ?? [];
    if (items.length === 0) return [];
    const line = (name: string, qty: number): string =>
      ["", label(`Unpriced items (${items.length})`), "", "", "", "", "", "", ""].join(",");
    const itemLine = (item: { name: string; quantity: number }): string =>
      ["", label(`  ${item.name}`), "", "", "", "", "", item.quantity.toFixed(2), ""].join(",");
    return [
      line("", items.length),
      ...items.map(itemLine),
    ];
  }

  const closingDebit = new Decimal(row.closingDebit);

  return 0;

/**
 * The account's balance as a debit-positive figure, so its side follows from
 * the sign: assets and expenses sit Dr, revenue and liabilities Cr.
 */
function debitValue(row: TrialBalanceRow, kind: CustomReportKind, opening = false): number {
  const period = kind === "PL" && !opening;
  const debit = new Decimal(period ? row.debit : opening ? row.openingDebit : row.closingDebit);
  const credit = new Decimal(period ? row.credit : opening ? row.openingCredit : row.closingCredit);
  return toStorage(debit.minus(credit));
